# xcquinox/alec -- development & improvement history

This file is the canonical record of every methodology- or results-affecting change to the
`xcquinox/alec/` research subpackage, and the source for the paper's methods/development narrative.

**Logging rule (repo convention):** every such change -- features; physics / loss / descriptor /
training-scheme changes; data, benchmark, or reference-value changes; basis / grid / density-fitting
changes; and any results-changing bug fix -- gets an entry here in the *same* change, recording the date,
commit short-hash, WHAT changed, and **Why:** the rationale. Keep the commit-message body consistent with
the entry. Pure scaffolding / formatting / test-only commits may be grouped into one line or omitted.

Dates are authoring dates; hashes are short git hashes (verifiable via `git show <hash>`).

---

## Phase 1 -- Package scaffolding, config/dataclass design, descriptors and constraints (2026-04-12 to 2026-04-13)

- 2026-04-12 `f9e38cfae` -- scaffolded the empty `xcquinox/alec/` package (workers + tests dirs); `add3309cf` added the shared pytest conftest with a JAX-x64 session fixture. **Why:** establish a self-contained research subpackage with float64 enforced everywhere (precision matters for sub-kcal/mol XC losses).
- 2026-04-12 `64d337c2a`, `8d395b59f`, `f9d2b953c` -- frozen-dataclass config layer: `FeatureSpec`/`_FrozenDict`, `ArchitectureConfig`, the `ARCHITECTURES` registry of 12 notebook-matching variants. **Why:** a hashable, serializable, validation-bearing config so jit cache keys stay stable and specs round-trip to JSON.
- 2026-04-12 `57c32ca70` -- `Descriptor` ABC + registry with the two foundational descriptors `cusp` and `dm_statistics`. **Why:** the descriptor set is the physical input to the GGA functional beyond `(rho, sigma)`.
- 2026-04-12 `bd101de40` -- `Constraint` ABC + registry + 4 constraints (Lieb-Oxford bound, UEG limit, non-negative correlation, etc.). **Why:** physical constraints are enforced architecturally, not just via the loss.
- 2026-04-12 `f31c4b90c`, `3c06bbd4b` -- `AlecGGA_XNet`/`AlecGGA_CNet` + `create_network_pair`; composed `AlecGGAModel` with `eval_exc`/`constraint_report`. `d936d941b`/`058739d49` added `MoleculeData` + `precompute_fixed_density_data` and `oneshot.py`. **Why:** the X/C network split lets exchange and correlation carry distinct descriptor inputs and constraints; one-shot fixed-density evaluation is the cheap training/eval path.
- 2026-04-12 `f650d0ab2` -- `AlecLoss` base + registry + 6 losses (A/B/C/D1/D2/D3); `37d81765f`/`003ed6a1b`/`57d6d2400` added `pretrain.py`, `train.py` (custom loop), `evaluation.py` (Metric ABC + 4 metrics). **Why:** the loss family spans atomization-energy, density-matrix, grid-density and combined objectives; pretraining warm-starts the nets to PBE before task training.
- 2026-04-12 `0ff180116`, `dddbf775b` -- `parallel.py` (WorkerJob/run_workers) + pretrain/train/test workers with thread-env-before-jax ordering. **Why:** local multiprocess parallelism; env vars must be set before JAX captures backend defaults.
- 2026-04-13 (step-4 notebook, ~25 commits `36940ccb3` ... `1eec66e68`, smoke test `fe3711dc2`) -- generated the step-4 training-example notebook cell-by-cell with tqdm progress and skip-if-exists toggles. **Why:** a reproducible end-to-end driver; long loops must show progress.

## Phase 2 -- Atom-energy anchor semantics, manual SCF backend, OEP (2026-04-14 to 2026-04-19)

- 2026-04-14 `367695856` -- wired the caller-supplied `atom_energies` anchor dict into all 6 loss `__call__`s. **Why:** the AE losses had been fed molecule indices instead of energy anchors after a signature rename, silently nonsensing the loss; this matches `AtomizationEnergyMetric` so train and eval use identical anchors.
- 2026-04-14 `2f8e5e4d2`/`2a5f2db80` -- removed a dead `_unpack_loss_state` helper (latent None-model bug); enforced PBE-consistent anchors. **Why:** keep train/eval AE definitions identical.
- 2026-04-16 (manual SCF backend, ~30 commits) -- `SolverBackend`/`SolverMode`/`FeaturePolicy` enums (`9276832ff`), `SolverConfig` (`4354b598a`), `Mixer` ABC + `LinearMixer` (`2899991ec`), `ConvergenceCriterion` + `EnergyConvergence` (`d3085bf38`), `SCFResult` + `run_scf` (`e5a1f3620`); ONESHOT / FIXED_J / FULL modes with a `jax.lax.scan` body and `REASSEMBLE` feature policy (`6befc10f9`, `cde56cca9`, `c907e8998`, `e9df38899`). **Why:** v1 ships `LinearMixer` + ABCs; FULL mode rebuilds the Fock per cycle so features must reassemble (else the NN sees stale features that disagree with J[D]); gradients must flow through the multi-cycle SCF for self-consistent training.
- 2026-04-16 `9bc7c4e42`/`e6b71225f`/`e1b1ee338` -- threaded `solver_config` through specs + all 6 losses; `d5a8a3ab0` adds `'eri'` to required precompute keys for FULL mode. **Why:** FULL mode recomputes J from the 4-index ERI each cycle; without it `run_manual_scf` crashes.
- 2026-04-16 `40f206e2e` -- contract test pinning one-shot energy == SCF total energy at the PBE density. **Why:** the cheap one-shot path must agree with the SCF fixed point at the reference density.
- 2026-04-17 (multi-task balancing, ~12 commits) -- `LossMetric` + `BalancingConfig` (`943a459cc`), relative-error DM/grid terms (`852b5c3a2`), `LossNorm` (`db6ac6080`), `TwoPhase` (`100878c91`), `GradNorm` (`712e5fa59`). **Why:** with V_xc/density/AE channels of wildly different magnitudes, fixed weights de-calibrate; balancing keeps under-trained channels alive.
- 2026-04-17 `61abaec61`/`83afa6058`/`3ae2078d3` -- `vxc_ref` on `MoleculeData` + an eigh-free `_vxc_term` + `vxc_weight` on all losses; `bb992c081`/`51aebcd0e` a Wu-Yang OEP inversion utility. **Why:** supervise the XC *potential* (not just energy) against an OEP-inverted reference; the eigh-free term avoids differentiating an eigendecomposition.
- 2026-04-18 to 04-19 (UKS spin-resolved path, ~20 commits) -- spin-resolved UKS Fock/DM (`d15ba4de4`), UKS FIXED_J/FULL branches (`fc1ce9c22`), corrected UKS `vxc_pbe` (subtract total J not per-spin) (`5bea7757f`), UKS E_xc from `veff.exc` (`313bcd691`), eliminated NaN grads on UKS atoms H/O (`4266cf6bd`) + guards (`15fedfbc2`), UKS OEP->`vxc_ref` npz integration (`73767a5a4`). **Why:** open-shell atoms (needed for atomization energies) drove `0*NaN` gradients through degenerate-eigenvalue eigh; UKS XC/J bookkeeping must follow PBE spin-scaling exactly.
- 2026-04-19 `6233543e4` -- bounded the cusp `log_weighted_Z` via `tanh(x/5)`. **Why:** prevent F_x saturation in descriptor-rich architectures.
- 2026-04-19 `847d9ec55` -- hardened the inner SCF against DIIS eigh failure on pathological BFGS steps. **Why:** the OEP outer optimizer probes V_xc points where the inner SCF can fail to diagonalize.

## Phase 3 -- PBE anchoring, integration-weighted pretraining, Round-2/3 physics review (2026-04-18 to 2026-04-28)

- 2026-04-18 `8f38a7997`/`d85d9c773` -- integration-weighted pretrain MSE via `PretrainSpec.loss_weighting` (default integration). **Why:** fitting F_x should minimize the *integrated* XC-energy residual, not a per-sample mean.
- 2026-04-21 `9de4739a3`/`ce606b1e1`/`857a6e27c` -- `PBEAnchorSample` + `pbe_anchor_loss` (UKS-aware, libxc targets, spin-scaled) + `pbe_anchor_weight` on all losses. **Why:** pin F_x toward the PBE shape so energy-only losses don't drift the large-s asymptote arbitrarily.
- 2026-04-21 (step-6 notebook, ~25 commits) -- Chakravorty atomic-energy dict (`4f6e0b8d8`), H2O/C2H2 W4-11-geometry CCSD + hardened OEP cells, a 72-spec subprocess-isolated training loop, transfer plots, F_x drift panels. **Why:** scale to a 3-group x multi-loss x multi-solver sweep on real W4-11 geometries with held-out transfer molecules.
- 2026-04-27 `533d7c45b` -- replaced the broken softmax channel-gate with real multi-head self-attention. **Why:** the "gate" was not attending; the `_attn` archs needed genuine per-grid attention.
- 2026-04-27 `27080e4a2` (major physics fix) -- corrected three review-caught errors: (1) `idempotency_error` was wrong by a factor of 2 for closed-shell RKS DMs (`DSD=2D`, Szabo-Ostlund 3.144), making the "correlation indicator" near-constant; fixed to the spin-orbital projector form (D'=D/2 RKS, per-spin UKS, Pople-Nesbet 1954). (2) `NonNegativeCorrelation` had an asymptotic floor `1-log2 ~= 0.307` instead of `F_c>=0` (Levy-Perdew); fixed to `softplus((F_raw-1)+log(e-1))`. (3) `CuspDescriptor` docstring mislabeled column 1; corrected to `tanh(log(sum Z_A/r_A)/5)`. **Why:** every model trained against the broken idempotency feature learned from a corrupted descriptor.
- 2026-04-27 `74ba6dde8` -- integration weights now include the Becke quadrature `dr_i`; UKS PBE pretrain targets spin-resolved (libxc `spin=1`); `pbe_anchor` falls back to analytic PBE F_x(s) at rho->0. **Why:** the prior integration mode minimized a weighted *mean* per-sample residual; on atomic Becke grids the weight varies ~6 decades, so omitting it de-calibrated the loss.
- 2026-04-27 `2a3f664ba` -- RKS DM rebuild switched to occupation-mask form `2*(C*occ)@C.T` for gradient stability under degenerate eigenvalues (C2H2/HCN/C2H4 pi-MOs); low-rho JVP guard; `MIXER_REGISTRY`; rejected the incoherent `(FULL, FROZEN)` config. **Why:** reverse-mode through eigh at degenerate shells produced spurious huge gradients.
- 2026-04-27 `6b916d5b5` -- OEP rewrite to the Wu-Yang JCP 118 (2003) displacement form `V_xc = V_xc^baseline + sum_t b_t g_t` with V-space Tikhonov regularization (Heaton-Burgess PRL 98, 2007) and full provenance in the npz. **Why:** start from a physical KS density (b=0=baseline), not a Hartree-only pathology; basis-independent regularization; downstream loaders can validate.
- 2026-04-27 `be7e7d84a` -- fixed three sites passing the spin-summed total DM into `compute_dm_features` for UKS molecules (forcing the RKS branch); fixed UKS one-shot DM to occupation-mask form; JIT-tracer guard. **Why:** the summed DM does not satisfy `DSD=2D`, so every UKS molecule produced a meaningless `idempotency_error`.
- 2026-04-27 `b11c06d47` -- `_dm_term` normalizes by element count (n_ao^2 RKS, 2*n_ao^2 UKS) for an RKS<->UKS-invariant per-element MSE; uniform `molecules_only` gate. **Why:** the UKS branch was off by a factor of 2 vs RKS.
- 2026-04-28 `38332eccd` -- moved `JAX_PLATFORMS=cpu`/`JAX_ENABLE_X64=1` setdefault to conftest module-load time. **Why:** the session-fixture form ran *after* jax initialized its backend, so the suite silently ran on GPU and "hung" thrashing cuSolver.
- 2026-04-28 `28560fe4e` -- closed a round-3 review (OEP Hermitian symmetrization, criterion registry, single-source SCF constants, mixer-state-frozen-after-convergence). **Why:** consolidate findings; single source of truth for SCF constants.
- 2026-04-28 `6261f8ded` -- `_dm_term` uses `math.prod(shape)` not `int(jnp.prod(...))`. **Why:** the jnp form returns a 0-d tracer and `int(tracer)` raises `ConcretizationTypeError` inside the jit step.
- 2026-04-28 `80814a00d` -- enabled `jax_enable_x64=True` in the `_train_one_spec` subprocess. **Why:** it was the only entry point silently inheriting float32; losses below 1e-7 lose precision in fp32, capping convergence.
- 2026-04-28 `a9c251ad4` -- bumped step counts (pretrain 200->1000, short 45->100, long 125->250); added the `L5_gradnorm_vxc` spec (B_atomization_plus_dm + dm_weight=0.1, vxc_weight=0.01, GradNorm alpha=1.5). **Why:** the short budget couldn't move the V_xc residual; GradNorm (Chen et al. 2018, ICML) tracks per-task gradient magnitudes to keep V_xc on par with the fast-falling AE; alpha=1.5 is the paper's robust value.
- 2026-04-28 `f893e3058` -- verified Lieb-Oxford enforcement: 0/90 specs violate F_x > 1.804; the ceiling is `eqx.field(static=True)`. **Why:** confirmed the LOB is architectural (`_AlecLOB`: `1.804*sigmoid(x-log0.804)`); apparent under-saturation is the F_x(s->inf) asymptote, which the LOB does not pin.

## Phase 4 -- Cross-sweep analysis library (2026-04-28 to 2026-04-30)

- 2026-04-28 `135555773` -- `comparison_lib.py` with 5 cited plot generators (baseline reduction; density-vs-energy tradeoff after Medvedev Science 355 (2017); in-dist-vs-transfer generalization gap; loss-strategy heatmap; arch comparison). **Why:** turn the 90-spec sweep into a publishable Pareto/baseline analysis with primary-source citations.
- 2026-04-28 `27ac348df` -- corrected two report claims unsupported by the data + regression-pinned them. **Why:** had reasoned from GradNorm/attention *design intent* not observed numbers; under `vxc_weight=0.01` GradNorm *down-weights* V_xc when its gradient is small (L5 lands in the no-V_xc density cluster), and attention is loss-dependent.
- 2026-04-30 `7842205bd`, `0194f6caa`, `357aa4490` -- step-5 origin comparison + combined step{5,6}x{unweighted,integration} synthesis; `357aa4490` fixed an apples-vs-oranges transfer comparison. **Why (finding):** integration pretraining universally tightens F_x (~3-4x) into a better basin, but pretrain weighting *does not close the generalization gap* -- training-set diversity is the bottleneck (a recurring theme).

## Phase 5 -- Step-7: subset selection, DFS pool, BH76/IP13 channels, CCSD ref pipeline (2026-05-01 to 2026-05-03)

- 2026-05-01 `c9834d802` ... `1c66d2a1f` -- `subset_selection.py`: `compute_descriptor_triple` (PBE 1996 s, SCAN 2015 alpha), `metric_l2`/`metric_jsd` (Lin 1991), reference-histogram binning, exhaustive `select_subset`. **Why:** choose a training subset whose descriptor distribution best covers a target, to study how training-set composition drives generalization.
- 2026-05-01 `69f5b92b1`/`70c0f37ef` -- `build_dick_pool` (28 points per Dick & Fernández-Serra 2021 SI §II), then renamed `dick_*`->`dfs_*` (citations preserved). **Why:** adopt the DFS training set as the canonical pool.
- 2026-05-01 `359485329`/`528482178` -- registered `L5_gradnorm_vxc_step7` as a 5-channel GradNorm loss (`loss_AE`, `loss_BH76` reaction-energy, `loss_IP13`, `loss_vxc`, `loss_rho`). **Why:** broaden supervision beyond atomization to barrier-height reactions and ionization potentials.
- 2026-05-01 `d1814c7e4`/`99cb7e203` -- BH76 forward barriers + IP13 IPs + W4-11/CCCBDB AE references for the 21 Dick AE molecules, with citations. **Why:** literature reference values, no fabrication.
- 2026-05-02 (CCSD reference pipeline, ~30 commits) -- 3-stage per-species precompute: SCF cache (`2f9da9412`), CCSD with spin-summed `rho_ref_grid` (`4794458e7`), OEP cascade (2-tier svp-jkfit/tzvp-jkfit) (`254b2c309`), with `preflight_uks_oep`, atomic kill-safe `RunLog`, `precompute_all`. **Why:** generate density + V_xc references for the full pool once, cached, crash-recoverable.
- 2026-05-02 `70ee5976d`/`781336bf9`/`6ce79d5b0` -- inner-SCF `level_shift` (HO/CN/NO Pi-state OEP basin-hopping), per-spin OEP `conv_tol` (UKS=1e-2, RKS=2e-3), L-BFGS early-stop on `conv_tol`. **Why:** open-shell radicals' OEP would basin-hop; UKS needs a looser tolerance than RKS.

## Phase 6 -- OEP plateau detection, per-species overrides, mixed-pool refactor, fast subset selection (2026-05-06 to 2026-05-10)

- 2026-05-06 (OEP plateau handling, ~12 commits) -- `_OEPPlateau` sentinel + `_detect_plateau` (F_val + density-error deques with sign-of-trend slack), `OEPResult.terminated_by`/`dm_final`. **Why:** the displacement-form OEP can plateau above tol; detect it rather than burning the full budget.
- 2026-05-06 (per-species OEP overrides, ~10 commits) -- `_PER_SPECIES_OEP_OVERRIDES` + `_validate_overrides` + `_resolve_tiers_for_species`; grid_level baked into intermediates cache filenames with crash-recovery migration; 8 discovered overrides pasted (`4975302ba`). **Why:** 6-8 species (Be, C+, F2, F2O, N2O, O3) need bespoke OEP tiers; the cache must be grid-keyed so a grid change doesn't reuse stale references.
- 2026-05-06 (HPC harness shell, ~8 commits) -- CLI shell with `--dry-run`, YAML loader, trial enumerator, SIGALRM wall-cap, per-trial OEP runner + DM-observable computation, verifier CLI with selection rule + plots. **Why:** drive the per-species OEP-override discovery sweep on SLURM and auto-select the winning tier.
- 2026-05-07 `491085309` -- mixed-pool `TrainingPoint` abstraction (21 AE + 3 BH76 + 2 IP13) with H/Li-only Dick atom anchors. **Why:** unify heterogeneous targets into one 26-point pool with a single atom-anchor scope.
- 2026-05-09 `f5b699498` -- prebinned + BLAS-batched `select_subset` (~1000x: C(26,12)=9.66M combos from ~34 h to 110 s). **Why:** histograms are additive over disjoint samples, so candidate densities are exact sums evaluated as a batched GEMM instead of re-binning per candidate.
- 2026-05-10 `eceb0a05a` -- split `_gradnorm_step` into `1+n_tasks+1` small JITs. **Why:** the monolithic step OOMed at ~31 GiB (5 channels x backprop-through-3-SCF-cycles kept 6 forward passes alive); the split drops peak to ~5 GiB and compile to ~30 s, GradNorm semantics bit-identical.
- 2026-05-10 `c68987721`/`5632318c9` -- `aux_only_names` excludes BH76 polyatomics from the AE channel; `build_indices` prefers neutral atoms over cations. **Why:** BH76 reactant species (HO, CH3, N2, F2) entered specs with `target=0.0` placeholders, collapsing the relative-error denominator to 1e-8 so a ~0.5 Ha residual blew up to ~2.5e7; and a `Li+` spec processed after neutral `Li` overwrote `atom_mol_idx[Li]`, training the cation toward the neutral anchor (~5 eV IP bias).

## Phase 7 -- BH76 reaction-energy realignment, HPC 4/5-stage harness, polarized correlation, forensic-review fixes (2026-05-19 to 2026-05-29)

- 2026-05-19 `341340a34`/`a2add7b5f` -- `bh76_mode` toggle defaulting to `reaction_energy`. **Why:** the BH76 term computes `sum(coeffs*E)` = a reaction energy, but `DFS_BH76_REACTIONS` stored *barrier heights*; the default now attaches the true GMTKN55-BH76RC reaction energies (`barrier_height` mode raises until TS geometries are staged).
- 2026-05-19 (HPC harness build-out, ~20 commits) -- `grid_config`, `domain.py` (physics tables), `materialize.py` (atomic spec serialization + manifest), `spec_builder.py`, `inputs.py` (consume-only subset-ledger staging), `submit.py` + sbatch templates for a 3-stage SLURM graph, `_preflight.py`, `_train_task`, `_eval_one_spec`, `job_tracking` (`jobs.json` + `reduce_outcomes`), `__main__` CLI. `a0f736742`/`d6d3c0a3e` re-scoped `prepare_inputs` to consume-only. **Why:** move training off the local box (caps at r=5) onto SLURM with a dependency graph and job-outcome classification.
- 2026-05-24 `46d3c791d` -- wired pretrain into a 4-stage graph (datagen->pretrain->preflight->train->eval). **Why:** pretraining is a per-arch SLURM stage; preflight consumes-only.
- 2026-05-24 `babd7aff8` (physics-correctness review) -- cited fixes: split UKS exchange/correlation spin-scaling (exchange per Oliver-Perdew PRA 20 (1979); correlation on total density per von Barth-Hedin 1972 / PW92); one-sided Lieb-Oxford floor 0 (Dick 2021 eq. 11); v_sigma mask 1e-10->1e-30 (open-shell energy<->potential residual 0.92 -> 2.3e-7); natural-occupation transform (Löwdin 1955); `dm_entropy` relabel. **Why:** the FD energy<->potential consistency was broken for open-shell systems.
- 2026-05-24 `dc6cc9b49` -- GradNorm neutralizes channels with step-0 loss `<=1e-8`. **Why:** `r_i=L_i(t)/L_i(0)` is undefined when a channel's step-0 loss is ~0 (BH76-only/IP13-only subsets); a 0->nonzero excursion gave `r~1e10` and corrupted all task weights.
- 2026-05-24 (P2-03 polarized correlation, ~10 commits `b23391191` ... `3d63ece65`) -- verified spin-polarized PW92 baseline (`pw92c_polarized_scalar`, matches libxc to 3e-10), gated `use_polarized_correlation` (default off; cnet gains Dick's bounded spin feature `x1 = 1/2[(1+zeta)^{4/3}+(1-zeta)^{4/3}]`), zeta threaded through the model, per-spin UKS correlation potential, `split_exc_energy_uks`, the manual UKS Fock, the pyscfad UKS callback, and cnet pretraining. **Why:** GGA correlation was evaluated at zeta=0 with no spin DOF, so spin-polarized atom energies were outside the model class (the dominant AE error); `utils.pw92c`'s polarized branch was dead. Default off is byte-identical so RKS-era checkpoints are unaffected.
- 2026-05-24 `2c9a976f3`/`444f9788d` -- per-descriptor selection weights (opt-in `descriptor_weights={'alpha':0.0}`); linear-scale PMF-normalized binning. **Why:** the GGA model is structurally blind to tau, yet meta-GGA `alpha` was 1/3 of the selection objective -- optimizing a coordinate the functional cannot represent.
- 2026-05-24 `98f8f0a55`/`145cccdb1` -- realigned the 3 training BH76 reaction energies to GMTKN55-BH76RC (+64.91/-5.44/+103.28). **Why:** GMTKN55 is the source of truth DFS used; values differ ~0.13-0.25 kcal/mol. *Checkpoint-affecting (changes training targets); approved 2026-05-24.*
- 2026-05-24 `8a14cf80f`/`7c748355c` -- fail-fast on a positive `pbe_anchor_weight` reaching L5 (and at submit). **Why:** L5 has no PBE-anchor channel and step-7 freezes pretraining, so the weight was silently swallowed.
- 2026-05-25 `8a6ba19db` -- moved constraint enforcement from `AlecGGAModel` into the X/C-net forward. **Why:** `run_pretrain` trained the bare networks, so pretraining was constraint-blind and a pretrained+constrained net regressed at eval; serialization stays byte-compatible (constraints are static).
- 2026-05-25/26 `a527499fe` ... `8a89c084f` (GMTKN55 constraint+pretraining demo) -- unconstrained baseline + increasing constraints (16-seed sweeps + constraint-aware pretrained). **Why (finding):** constraints+pretraining improve W4-11 atomization *only for the polarized baseline* -- AE isolates open-shell atoms, so the zeta-blind model has irreducible error and pretraining/constraints "commit harder to the wrong manifold."
- 2026-05-26 (self-consistency ladder, ~8 commits) -- `multimode_constraint_eval.py` evaluates through fixed-rho / one-shot / 3-step SCF, with a truly-unconstrained (`lob_lim=None`) pretrain override. **Why (finding):** 3-step SCF barely moves the numbers vs fixed-rho, so fixed-rho conclusions are robust.
- 2026-05-26 `9e60c2eb8`/`f94f3cbf6` -- `submit --polarized` + an arch-auto-selected zeta-aware `pretrain_data_polarized.npz` (per-grid `zeta_all`; fail-fast if absent). **Why:** a polarized run pretrains its own polarized cnets for a clean A/B; never warm-start a polarized net at zeta=0.
- 2026-05-29 `4def50d14` (forensic-review fixes + 8-arch ablation) -- three gated flags (default off preserves pickle round-trip): **`dm_entropy_intensive`** normalizes entropy by `ln(n_orb_eff)` (the pre-fix extensive `ln(N_occ)` form *leaked molecule-size info to every grid point* -- the root cause the 2026-05-29 forensic review traced "polarized doesn't beat unpolarized + r=0 train<->held-out" to); **`descriptor_log_transform`** (Dick XCDiff `(1-exp(-x^2))*log(x+1)`); **`zero_init_final_layer`** (F_x=F_c=1 at init). Plus: `local_reeval` honors `solver_config` (eval matches train's supervision domain); registry 12->14 with `deep_notransform`(`_attn`) controls. **Why:** the forensic review's headline was a size-extensive `dm_entropy` label leak.
- 2026-05-29 `90b35080c` -- cluster eval scores every net against the *full* BH76 (76) + W4-11 (140) sets via `full_benchmark_pools` (GMTKN55->JSON caches) + `eval_holdout`. **Why:** an apples-to-apples comparison surface vs XCDiff and the standard published BH76+W4-11 tables.

## Phase 8 -- Self-consistent energy training, density fitting, geometry-units fix, held-out correctness (2026-05-31 to 2026-06-05)

- 2026-05-31 `bd647ebae` (geometry-units bug) -- `_atoms_to_pyscf_str` had divided already-angstrom `struc.xyz` coords by `BOHR_PER_ANGSTROM`, shrinking every held-out molecule ~1.89x and garbaging all BH76/W4-11 reaction energies (NN *and* PBE). Read as angstrom; rebuilt JSON caches. **Why:** corrected BH76 PBE MAE to 11.82 (was 182); training was never affected (separate g2_97 angstrom source). Added per-SCF-cycle energy-trace capture.
- 2026-06-01 `967223a7a` -- held-out eval reads `basis`/`grid_level` from config instead of hardcoding def2-svp. **Why:** prerequisite for any basis change; eval had silently pinned def2-svp.
- 2026-06-01 (density fitting, ~10 commits `4ae6e85bc` ... `5b6bda959`) -- pipeline-wide DF: differentiable DF-Coulomb + `build_cderi`, `SolverConfig.density_fit`+`auxbasis` (default off), `cderi` precompute, DF in the manual SCF, DF PBE/HF/CCSD reference generation, basis-/DF-tagged caches across pretrain/train/refs. **Why:** full-ERI (no DF) caps the basis at ~def2-svp; DF is required to reach def2-tzvpd+.
- 2026-06-01 `bf19c862a`/`9b086aad8` -- matched the pretrain + pyscfad-eval cusp `log_transform` to training. **Why:** the cusp descriptor used a different transform at pretrain/eval than at train, skewing the feature; requires pretrain-data regen + cusp-arch retrain.
- 2026-06-01 `a10c9eef1` (self-consistent energy training -- root cause) -- `oneshot.total_energy_for_solver` as the single source of truth: FULL mode -> `run_scf().total_energy` (gradient through the 3 SCF cycles via `lax.scan`); ONESHOT/FIXED_J/None -> one-shot fixed-density energy; `losses`/`evaluation`/`eval_holdout` all route through it. **Why:** the full_3 SCF ran and was differentiable, but the energy/AE loss *discarded* it (`_compute_energies` did `del solver_config` and always returned the one-shot energy on the frozen PBE density) -- so the intended backprop-through-SCF self-consistent training never happened. Now training optimizes exactly what eval measures.
- 2026-06-01 `92ffcf94c`/`4b840ff22` (DFS per-molecule scheme) -- `update_scheme='per_molecule'` (new cluster default): decompose the spec into per-target groups, shuffle each epoch, one optimizer step per group with FIXED density-dominant channel weights (dpyscf-style, `loss_rho` 20x the energy channels). **Why:** breaks the full-batch gradient-averaging compromise that pinned multi-target AE fits to a ~5% plateau while a single target fit perfectly.
- 2026-06-01 `d99badd01` -- added a `datagen` stage as the first (dependency-free) node; pretrain gates on `afterok:datagen`. **Why:** pretrain-data generation had lived inside `prepare_inputs` (preflight, `afterok:pretrain`) -- *after* the stage that consumes it -- so pretrain raised `FileNotFoundError` and the chain went `DependencyNeverSatisfied`.
- 2026-06-01 `e8e412e88`/`c0eb5bda4` -- parallelized held-out eval across molecules with an adaptive-degradation ladder + queue-agnostic CPU detection (default-on). **Why:** held-out eval over 214 species per spec was the throughput bottleneck.
- 2026-06-01 `8d1b92a5d`/`4b1ae3fb0`/`8a282c1c5` -- parallel exhaustive subset selector + GPU JSD selector with on-device combinadic unranking + a BH76+W4-11 representative-subset sweep. **Why:** generate held-out-aware "representative" subsets maximizing descriptor overlap with the full BH76+W4-11 distribution; verified parallel == serial.
- 2026-06-01 `eb8f1e1d8`/`e70afc595` -- generalizable BH76+W4-11 trainable pool + `bh76w411_step7` domain + svp/grid2 and tzvpd/grid2+DF submissions (48 specs each, `held_out_strict`). **Why:** train on representative subsets of the full benchmark with two basis/DF settings for a cross-basis study.
- 2026-06-02 `c941b99c5`/`3d6cad225` -- extended `ATOMIC_ENERGIES_CHAKRAVORTY` with Be/B/Al/Si/P/Cl (Chakravorty 1993 PRA 47 Table XI). **Why:** the bh76w411 pool references 12 elements but the table had 8; `TrainingSpec.validate` aborted preflight (SLURM 54403) after ~1.5 h of CCSD-ref generation, leaving the chain `DependencyNeverSatisfied`. A CI regression now fails in ms.
- 2026-06-02 `f06f46372`/`f07fddac4` -- opt-in `SolverConfig.scf_grad_checkpoint` (jax.checkpoint on both SCF scan bodies) + a DF auxbasis correctness fix. **Why:** `precompute` built `cderi` without forwarding auxbasis, and `default_auxbasis('def2-tzvpd')` returned None, so pyscf silently auto-selected an aux basis -- training cderi then disagreed with the CCSD references; now def2-* -> def2-universal-jkfit (Weigend PCCP 8, 2006), threaded everywhere and in the cache key.
- 2026-06-02 `96666fd6b` (cluster/DF review batch) -- 10 re-verified fixes: out-of-core `build_cderi` via `dfobj.loop()` (the `np.asarray(_cderi)` crashed when pyscf spilled to HDF5); held-out AE refs from `eval_molecules` not `training_spec.molecules` (every held-out compound was silently unscored); `bh76_mode='barrier_height'` raises; inline-eval SIGTERM trap; `inline_eval` round-trip persistence; a Hartree-units guard (>10 Ha raises, catching ~627x kcal/mol errors); partial `channel_weights` fill from defaults (not 1.0, which de-emphasized `loss_rho` from 20). **Why:** consolidate the review findings on the DF training path; 285 tests green.
- 2026-06-03 `074372c37` (polarized-correlation NaN root cause + fix) -- clip zeta to ±(1-1e-6) in energy and potential paths, floor PW92 `fz`, fail-loud `_abort_if_nonfinite` in all 5 train loops. **Why:** bh76w411 svp/tzvpd_df trained to all-NaN; `use_polarized_correlation` + fully-polarized atoms (H/Li, rho_beta=0, zeta=±1) drove an `inf` 2nd-derivative through FULL SCF. Shipped because live tests are ONESHOT-only; confirmed via repro + cluster `aux_log.pkl`.
- 2026-06-03 (round-3 cleanup, `60e3bdddc` ... `615eb6b87`) -- deleted dead `utils.pw92c()`, fixed a pool-cache `is`->`==` identity bug, corrected `dm_entropy` divisor + cusp-envelope comments, renamed `ScalingSymmetric s2`->`s2_unnormalized`; fixed a DF-CCSD crash on empty-spin-channel species (H atom, `noccb=0` -> non-DF CCSD), added a cf4 OEP override, gave the pyscfad sigma/feature-response tests a non-zero-init model. **Why:** clear round-3 findings; untrained nets are zero-init (F_c=1, zero input-grad) so feature-response tests need a non-trivial model.
- 2026-06-04 `51cb947aa`/`3f9bcef19` (held-out correctness) -- molecule-level, charge-aware (anions are molecules), case-insensitive overlap detection; `summaries` pull filter includes `eval_holdout/`. **Why:** three train/held-out overlap leaks (atom-decimation, anion-as-atom `f-`/`cl-`, case-variant `CH4`/`ch4`); the garbage W4-11 AEs were a *separate* undertraining verdict (SCF divergence), not an eval bug.
- 2026-06-05 `378c2455c` -- corrected the cusp docstring attribution: the `-2Z` spherically-averaged *density* cusp is Steiner (JCP 39, 1963), not Kato (CPAM 10, 1957, the `-Z` *wavefunction* cusp). **Why:** comment-only; the `exp(-2Zr)` factor (density decays at twice the wavefunction rate) is unchanged.
- 2026-06-05 `ac07c6aa2` -- save a best-loss `model_best.eqx` alongside the final across all training loops. **Why:** eval used the *final* checkpoint, so a late-instability arc (deep_attn ss6) scored its diverged endpoint; a best-checkpoint pass isolates instability from generalization.
- 2026-06-05 `5a4b3cc48`/`9fb240600` (failure-mechanism diagnostics) -- `classify_failures`/`_classify_cell` label each cell from held-out MAE vs PBE + final-window loss into {pass, generalization_gap, late_instability}; cumulative both-bases training-loss plot. **Why (finding):** deep_attn ss6 is the *only* late-instability cell (final loss 7e-2, 19x cohort median); the other 18 failures are generalization gaps (clean 2-8e-3 final loss, overfit concentrated on W4-11).
- 2026-06-05 (methods-box sourcing arc, `28bead118` ... `e6ca9636b`) -- a fully-sourced expert methods box, every equation carrying `[n]` or an honest "this work", cross-checked every equation against its primary source. Code-verified corrections: C-net `LOB_2.0` is a [0,2] squash (not an LO bound on F_c); `dm_entropy` intensive `/ln max(n_orb_eff,2)`; the loss is *not* GradNorm-balanced at run time (both runs set `update_scheme=per_molecule`, so GradNorm(alpha=1.5) is dormant; effective weights `{AE:1,BH76:1,IP13:1,vxc:1,rho:20}`); energy/density = final state of a fixed 3-cycle differentiable KS SCF, V_xc one-shot; W4-11 atomizations train as *reactions* through the BH76 channel; the `1.804` ceiling = PBE `1+kappa` vs DFS's tighter `1.174` (Gedanken JCP 140 (2014)); BH76/W4-11 refs are W2-F12 (not CCSD(T)). **Why:** singular-source-of-truth for an expert audience; ours is a *GGA reduction* of the DFS meta-GGA (no `x3=alpha`, `r_s` for density, `tanh^2(s)` UEG gate, global vs local LOB), stated honestly.

## Phase 10 -- generalization-gap decoupling fixes (2026-06-20)

Following the 2026-06-20 review (compound generalization gap: over-capacity nets + no L2 + no
validation/early-stop; see `reports_local/dfs_discrepancy_review_2026-06-20.md`), the first three independent,
low-risk decoupling fixes (existing decay-free runs stay byte-identical -- all new knobs default off):

- 2026-06-20 `ac4de863d` -- 8 depth-3/width-16 `deep_*_3x16` ARCHITECTURES twins of the dfs_step7 sweep archs (config.py), each mirroring its 4x32 sibling's flags. **Why:** our 4x32 nets (~3.3k params/net) overfit the 26-point DFS pool; DFS used 3 hidden layers x 16 nodes (~0.6k). Capacity reduction is the #1-ranked decoupling fix.
- 2026-06-20 `ac4de863d` -- `build_optimizer` adam->adamw with a threaded `weight_decay` hyperparameter (YAML hyperparams -> HyperParams -> TrainingSpec -> all 6 optimizer call sites; NOT pretrain, which would fight the PBE warm-start). **Why:** we trained with no weight decay while DFS uses L2 (og_dpyscf/scripts/train.py:47,289); `weight_decay=0` is byte-identical to the former adam.
- 2026-06-20 `ac4de863d` -- threaded `scf_grad_checkpoint` through the grid `SolverNamed` / `_build_solvers` / `_solver_config_from_named` so a named solver (full_25) can enable jax.checkpoint on the unrolled SCF scan. **Why:** 25-cycle FULL-SCF backprop (a DFS-fidelity ceiling fix) needs O(sqrt(cycles)) checkpointed memory; the per-molecule scheme keeps it feasible (~8x walltime, not RAM).

- 2026-06-20 `a8f0c45a7` (WS3) -- held-out val/test split (`eval_holdout.split_held_out`, deterministic md5-hash partition) + in-loop early-stop on a DISJOINT validation slice + validation-best checkpoint (`model_val_best.eqx`) in the `per_molecule` loop; the held-out eval reports ONLY the TEST slice, gated on the spec's ACTUAL validation activation (validate_every>0 AND validation_molecules AND validation_reactions_path), with a submit-time guard (`validate_grid_semantics`) requiring `val_refs_dir` + `update_scheme=='per_molecule'`. **Why:** the over-capacity nets drive TRAINING loss to ~0 while held-out generalization decouples; stopping + selecting on a disjoint val signal is the #2/#3-ranked decoupling fix. All knobs no-op at defaults (existing runs byte-identical). On independent re-derivation, 1 blocking val-test leakage defect + 3 cleanups were caught (fixed); 283 fast tests. The preflight val-SCF precompute was found dead (the runtime build does its own SCF, energy-only validation needs no targets) and removed; val density is rebuilt at train time, `val_reactions.json` staged in preflight.

- 2026-06-20 `498e35640` (WS5) -- resumable per-molecule training: periodic ATOMIC `resume_*` checkpoints (model + opt_state + np.random state + the in-place-shuffled group `order` permutation + both trackers incl. best-model snapshots + losses/aux) every `checkpoint_every` epochs, resume-on-start that continues exactly, a `completion.json` sentinel (written + `resume_*` deleted at clean-finish/early-stop), and a best-effort worker SIGTERM flush (parent waits up to 30s for it). **Why:** cluster maintenance/walltime kills were wasting whole runs (cf. the tzvpd maintenance outage). Contract with WS6: periodic checkpoints NEVER write `model.eqx` (the harness success signal), so mid-run = `resume_state.pkl` present + no `model.eqx`/`completion.json`. `checkpoint_every=0` (default) is a byte-identical no-op. On review, a blocker was caught -- the shuffled `order` wasn't persisted, so a >=2-group resumed run DIVERGED from an uninterrupted same-seed run (masked by a 1-group test fixture) -- plus a torn-flush major + 4 robustness gaps (try/finally flusher cleanup, guarded corrupt-checkpoint load, parent child.wait, orphan-resume handling); ALL fixed, multi-group resume-equivalence proven fails-before/passes-after to rtol 1e-9.

- 2026-06-20 `60a831a40` (WS6) -- harness completion/incomplete detection + resubmit-RESUME: `job_tracking._disk_outcome` gains `incomplete_resumable` (resume_state.pkl present + no model.eqx/completion.json), checked BEFORE the failure.json branch so a killed-mid-run task's `killed_by_signal` failure.json does not trigger a FRESH retry; `cmd_resubmit` routes it to a RESUME relaunch that does NOT archive the `resume_*` set (two independent guards), so the relaunched train task auto-continues; the per-index attempt cap still applies; `cmd_status` tallies `resumable` + a remedy line; `_eval_one_spec` emits a clear "training incomplete (resume in progress)" message when model.eqx is absent. Also fixed a WS4 regression surfaced by the full suite (the `test_df_memory` `_Named` stub lacked `scf_grad_checkpoint`). **Why:** the tzvpd maintenance outage wasted whole runs; with the WS5 checkpoints the harness now CONTINUES them instead of restarting/failing them as `dependency_never_satisfied`. Reviewed against intent: zero confirmed defects.

- 2026-06-20 `f48e5f1a3` (WS7) -- new submittable `hpcjobs/configs/dfs_step7.svp_grid2_v3.yaml`: the 8 depth-3/width-16 archs + adamw `weight_decay=1e-4` + held-out validation (`val_frac=0.2`, `validate_every=25`, `patience=5`) + resumable `checkpoint_every=10`, on the v2 regime (density per-electron, ae-as-reactions, lr 1e-3 / hold 50% / 500 epochs) -- a controlled v2->v3 generalization-gap A/B (88 specs). `full_25` (25-cycle FULL SCF + grad-checkpoint) is DEFINED but NOT swept (the ceiling-fix follow-up). The figure suite `ARCH_ORDER`/`ARCH_COLOR` learned the `*_3x16` archs (each twin reuses its 4x32 sibling's color; tab10 cap respected). Validated locally: `validate_grid_semantics` passes (the WS3 guard accepts validate_every>0 + val_refs_dir + per_molecule), 88 cells expand. **Why:** ship WS1/WS2/WS3/WS5 as ONE submittable experiment testing whether capacity + regularization close the held-out gap vs the existing 4x32 run. USER runs `python -m xcquinox.alec.cluster submit hpcjobs/configs/dfs_step7.svp_grid2_v3.yaml` (dry-run by default; add `--submit`).

- 2026-06-21 `ce049199c` (WS7 follow-up) -- `hpcjobs/configs/dfs_step7.svp_grid2_v3_full25.yaml`: the v3 decoupling job with `solver: [full_25]` (25-cycle FULL SCF + `scf_grad_checkpoint`), the DFS-fidelity SCF CEILING fix, distinct `output_root`. ~8x the full_3 per-step SCF compute, so it leans on the WS5/WS6 resumable harness (`checkpoint_every=10` + `resubmit --submit`) to span SLURM walltime windows. **Why:** ready-to-run follow-up once the v3 (full_3) A/B shows capacity+regularization shrink the held-out gap -- a more self-consistent NN density (3 cycles from converged PBE can't reach the NN's own fixed point) raises the achievable ceiling. Both configs validated locally (88 specs, `validate_grid_semantics` passes).

- 2026-06-22 `2a6266215` (v3 optimizer-update crash fix) -- all 4 model-update call sites (`_train_step`, `_normed_step`, GradNorm `_apply_updates`, per-molecule `_step`) now pass `_trainable_params(model)` = `eqx.filter(model, eqx.is_array)` as the optimizer `params`, not the raw model. **Why:** the WS2 `adam`->`adamw` switch (`ac4de863d`) made `optimizer.update` CONSUME its `params` arg via optax `add_decayed_weights` (`tree_map(g + wd*p, updates, params)`); the call sites still handed it the raw Equinox model, whose non-array leaves (`eqx.nn.MLP` `activation`=gelu + the default `final_activation` lambda, networks.py:113,258) are `None` in the `eqx.filter_value_and_grad` grads -- a tree-structure mismatch. Newer JAX (the cluster env) no longer treats `None` as a tree-prefix of a non-None leaf and raised `ValueError: Expected None, got <function <lambda>>`, failing ALL 88 `dfs_step7.svp_grid2_v3` train_eval specs in `_step` (`run_20260621T140311Z`; datagen/preflight/pretrain/refs all succeeded). Plain `adam` IGNORES its `params` arg, so the mismatch was latent before WS2 and harmless on older JAX (None-prefix tolerated) -- the v3 run is just the first on the post-adamw code. `_trainable_params` is the same filter every matching `optimizer.init` already uses, so `params` is now structurally identical to the grads; `eqx.apply_updates(model, updates)` is unaffected (it keeps the model's non-array leaves where the update is `None`). The pre-existing end-to-end training tests are `@pytest.mark.slow` (default `addopts = -m "not slow"`), so the default suite never exercised `optimizer.update` and CI stayed green -- two fast regression tests now do (a version-independent structural invariant + the real `_train_step` with function leaves), and the slow `per_molecule` end-to-end passes. USER: rsync `xcquinox/alec/train.py` to the cluster and `resubmit --submit` the v3 run (cached datagen/pretrain/CCSD-refs skip; nothing produced `model.eqx`, so it is a fresh train_eval relaunch, not a WS5/WS6 resume).

- 2026-06-22 `ec4996e70` (density/vxc double-supervision fix; from a source-level review) -- `_training_groups` now SKIPS the redundant `ae:<name>` group for any aux-forced compound (`m.name in aux_only_names`). **Why:** under `ae_as_reactions=true` (v3/v3_full25/v2) each reaction-form W4-11 AE compound -- and every BH76/IP13 polyatomic aux intermediate -- carries a REAL target (`spec_builder` injects `e_rxn_ref` into `ae_ref_kcalmol` -> `build_targets` returns a real, not placeholder, target) AND is forced into `aux_only_names`, so `_training_groups` created BOTH a `bh76:<name>` reaction group AND a separate `ae:<name>` group (`train.py:1187-1190` gated only on `m.name in targets`). The `ae:<name>` group has no AE term (aux-forced) but `_iter_idx_for_aux_channels` (`losses.py:1270-1282`) still returns the compound's index, so it re-applied density (weight 20) + vxc -- density/vxc-supervising those species TWICE per epoch, a ~2x over-weighting of the dominant density channel for exactly the 21 W4-11 compounds. Fixed-anchor AE compounds (`ae_as_reactions=false`, not aux) are unaffected (single `ae:<name>` group). Found + verified two ways (code trace + an empirical `_training_groups` run) by the 2026-06-22 source-level review (`reports_local/dfs_training_review_2026-06-22.md`, local-only; oracles = the pinned `og_dpyscf` source + DFS paper/SI + in-repo + internal consistency). The same commit corrects 3 stale/wrong docstrings the review flagged (BUG-2/3 + the stale BUG-1 docstring): the `training_points.py` predicted-atom-reaction docstring (claimed a 0.0 placeholder), the `_grid_term` provenance citation (`dpyscf/losses.py:171` -> `og_dpyscf/ogdpyscf/losses.py:62`), and the `_iter_idx_for_aux_channels` `HBPT` mislabel. The review otherwise CONFIRMED the core DFS-matching machinery faithful (adamw + `_trainable_params`, density N_e^2, AE-as-reactions, FULL-SCF energy, 3x16, validation) + 18 documented intentional deviations + 16 test/clarity gaps; source-level reading corrected the in-repo oracle itself (og default `rho_weight`=25 not 20; og `E_weight`/DFS lambda_E default 0.0, so the re-impl's absent absolute-energy anchor MATCHES the og source default; GELU activation confirmed matching). Regression test asserts an aux-forced reaction compound lands in exactly one density/vxc group. USER: this affects the in-flight v3 + full25 runs (`run_20260622T111908Z` / `run_20260622T111957Z`) -> scancel + rsync + resubmit clean.

## Phase 11 -- DFS SCF-trajectory tail loss + step-decaying mixer (2026-06-24)

- 2026-06-24 `<pending>` (full_25 collapse: root cause + DFS self-consistency fix) -- the `dfs_step7.svp_grid2_v3_full25` run (25-cycle FULL SCF) was catastrophically WORSE than `svp_grid2_v3` (3-cycle): held-out reaction MAE **75-110 kcal/mol vs 13-19**, i.e. 5-7x worse than the PBE baseline (~15), uniformly across all 18 matched specs. **Root cause:** training (`losses._compute_energies`) and eval (`eval_holdout.evaluate_holdout`) both scored ONLY the final SCF cycle's energy (`run_scf().total_energy`). For hard species (transition states, radicals) the NN-driven SCF, given 25 cycles, left the stable PBE-initialized basin and the constant linear mixer (alpha=0.5) could not damp it -- the captured per-cycle traces show a clean **period-2 oscillation** in the tail (e.g. `t-hooo` steps 18-24 alternate -223.40/-223.75/-223.32/-223.72/-223.31/-223.71/-223.30) or are still mid-drift (`s4-c2v` ends 10.3 Ha ~ 6500 kcal/mol off PBE). The final-step energy is then an arbitrary oscillation phase, poisoning both eval (+/-1000-1500 kcal/mol reaction outliers) and training (full25 final loss `8e-3` vs v3 `3.8e-8`, val MAE `212` vs `11.9`, no early-stop). Splitting held-out reactions by SCF convergence proves the mechanism: **converged** reactions MAE ~12-28 (~ v3); **non-converged** reactions MAE 140-485, and there are 26-59 non-converged species per spec. full_3 escapes only because 3 cycles is too few to leave the PBE basin (near-one-shot, stable but inert).

  **The fix (DFS / Dick & Fernandez-Serra 2021, verified verbatim in the pinned `og_dpyscf/og_dpyscf/ogdpyscf/`):** dpyscf forces many SCF cycles but (1) scores only the convergence TAIL with a quadratic weight rising toward convergence (`losses.py:14` `weights = linspace(0,1,N)**2`, `:18` `dE = dE[skip_steps:]`, `train.py:283` `skip_steps = max(5, scf_steps-10)` -> last 10 of 25; energy-only, `rho_loss` uses the final dm), and (2) uses a step-decaying mixer (`torch_routines.py:174-178` `alpha = 0.3**step + 0.3`). We port both:
  - **`solver.DecayingLinearMixer`** (registry `decaying_linear`): `alpha = base**step + floor` (DFS base=floor=0.3 -> step0=1.3 over-relaxation, settling to 0.3), reading the existing `MixerState.step_index` (frozen on convergence). NOT clamped (the step-0 over-relaxation is part of DFS). Damps the period-2 oscillation a constant mixer cannot.
  - **Per-step tail-weighted ENERGY loss** (DFS-faithful, the user's chosen fidelity): `oneshot.scf_tail_window(N, tail, power)` generalizes DFS's `skip = max(5, N-10)` (which underflows to an EMPTY slice for small N) to **any** `full_X` via `tail_len = min(N, tail)`, `skip = max(0, N - tail)` (so full_3, full_25, and everything between use the SAME scheme; DFS-exact at N=25, all-steps at N<=tail, with an N=1 guard). The energy-derived loss channels (`_ae_losses`, `_atomic_reg`, `_rxn_residual_term` (BH76), `_ip_residual_term` (IP13)) consume a per-species energy TRAJECTORY `(N, T)` from `oneshot.energy_trajectory_for_solver` / `losses._compute_energy_trajectories` and reduce each reaction/AE residual over the tail as DFS's `mean((w_i r_i)^2)`. Gated by new `SolverConfig.scf_loss_use_tail` / `scf_loss_tail` / `scf_loss_weight_power` (default OFF -> the per-step path collapses to T=1, w=[1] = the prior final-step loss, byte-identical; verified by the unchanged loss suites). Density/V_xc channels unchanged (DFS weights energy only).
  - **Convergence-aware REPORTING**: `oneshot.total_energy_for_solver` (so training's energy/AE metric + in-loop validation MAE) and `eval_holdout.evaluate_holdout` now report the tail-weighted MEAN energy when enabled (denoised; equals the converged value for a frozen tail), while still recording the raw final-cycle `total_energy` + the full `scf_energy_step_*` trace for forensics.
  - **Config plumbing**: `mixer_name`/`mixer_kwargs` + the three `scf_loss_*` knobs thread from a `solvers:` YAML entry through `SolverNamed` -> `_build_solvers` -> `_solver_config_from_named` -> `SolverConfig.describe()` (auto-logged to `train_metadata.json`). Both `hpcjobs/configs/dfs_step7.svp_grid2_v3.yaml` and `dfs_step7.svp_grid2_v3_full25.yaml` now enable `decaying_linear` (0.3/0.3) + `scf_loss_use_tail` (tail 10, power 2) on their `full_3` and `full_25` entries.

  TDD throughout (new tests in `test_solver.py`, `test_scf_tail.py`, `test_cluster_spec_builder.py`, `test_losses.py`, `test_eval_holdout.py`): the tail reducers, the mixer schedule, the DFS-exact window at N=25 + small-N/N=1 generalization, per-step residual correctness on synthetic oscillating traces, grad-flow through ALL tail steps, and the byte-identical off-path. **Why:** make the forced-multi-cycle FULL SCF actually trainable -- the final-step-only supervision had no pressure on the SCF trajectory to converge, so the NN learned a functional whose SCF is unstable for hard species; the DFS tail loss penalizes the oscillation directly and the decaying mixer helps it converge. **Confound noted (separate):** full25 resolved to n_steps=100 vs v3's 150 (both source YAMLs say 500) -- a resolve-time mapping to investigate before resubmission; not changed here. **USER:** rsync the changed `xcquinox/alec/*.py` + both YAMLs, then resubmit clean (changed solver + loss invalidate the existing full25 checkpoints -> fresh train, not a WS5/WS6 resume); verify the resolved n_steps.

The two sibling round-tripped fields already solved exactly this -- `_parse_channel_weights` and `_parse_pretrain_atoms` both accept a dict OR a list-of-pairs; `mixer_kwargs` was the lone new field that didn't follow the pattern. **Fix:** new `grid_config._parse_mixer_kwargs` mirrors those siblings (dict | list/tuple of pairs -> sorted hashable tuple; None/empty -> None); plus a `spec_builder._solver_config_from_named` hardening so a solver that selects a custom mixer but omits `mixer_kwargs` overrides to `()` (the mixer then uses its own `__init__` defaults) instead of inheriting `SolverConfig`'s default `{'alpha':0.5}`, which `DecayingLinearMixer` has no `alpha` arg for and would `TypeError` on in `_build_mixer`. TDD: `test_cluster_grid_config` reproduces the exact crash through the real `_config_to_raw_dict` + yaml round-trip (plus parser unit cases); `test_cluster_spec_builder` covers the mixer-default hardening; the round-trip was then verified end-to-end on both shipped v3 YAMLs. **Why:** the Phase 11 config plumbing was never exercised through the resolved-config RELOAD before shipping, so the dict->list round-trip (a known, already-solved hazard in this same file) slipped through; this unblocks the resubmit so the mixer + tail-loss fix can finally run. The pre-fix `MaxSignal [9]` / `ExitCode [0-1]` SLURM summaries are from EARLIER (lower-id) submissions on the OLD config (constant alpha=0.5 mixer, final-step-only loss) -- i.e. the pre-existing full_25 collapse this phase targets, NOT a regression from the new code. **USER:** rsync `xcquinox/alec/cluster/{grid_config,spec_builder}.py` (with the rest of the Phase 11 changes) and resubmit clean; the `resolved_config.yaml` files already on the cluster are now parseable, so a fresh submit is cleanest. **Resolves the n_steps confound flagged in the prior entry:** the full25=100 vs v3=150 difference is NOT a resolve-time bug -- it is the intentional `--n-steps` CLI override (`__main__.py:698`, `--n-steps` -> `hyperparams.n_steps`), which the 2026-06-25 resubmits passed explicitly (`--n-steps 100` full25 / `--n-steps 150` v3; the YAML's 500 is overridden by design -- fewer steps for the costlier 25-cycle SCF). Both configs fresh-submitted clean on 2026-06-25 (`run_20260625T191113Z`, datagen 65638; `run_20260625T191129Z`, datagen 2044343).
