THE REMOVAL IS ALSO A REPAIR: it took the `dm_statistics` architectures' energy/potential finite-difference residual from 1.04e-02 to 2.1e-10 under the committed test's own parametrized ordering (residuals move up to ~5x with evaluation order; a fresh-process measurement gave 5.2e-03 to the same floor), because the dead gradient had been dominating it -- so `deep_dm` and `deep_combined` now meet the same floor as every other architecture, and the `_TOL_BLOCKED_DM` tolerance branch that had been absorbing that residual was deleted along with the meta-GGA branch. Every architecture is now held to one measured bound. NEW DESCRIPTOR `rung35_multishell` (`deep_rung35ms_3x16`, additive; existing entries untouched): per-spin, per-width local occupancies `n_sigma(r; w) = A_w(r)^T P^sigma A_w(r)`, the RADIAL generalization of the localized density-matrix projection used by NeuralXC (Dick and Fernandez-Serra, *Nat. Commun.* 11, 3509 (2020)) and carried in the DFS reference implementation, which projects the density matrix onto a localized basis and contracts the coefficients into rotationally invariant per-shell norms. Linear in the density matrix, so differentiable with no eigendecomposition and no degeneracy hazard, and bounded in [0, 1] by the same Bessel argument as the single-width form. LIMITATION recorded because the name invites the stronger claim: `fakemol_for_charges` builds s-type projectors only, so this is the `l = 0` part of that construction -- with one m per shell the invariant `sqrt(sum_m c_{nlm}^2)` collapses to the occupancy itself, and angular channels need solid-harmonic fakemols. It is NOT "the DFS descriptor" until those exist. VERIFIED: bitwise identical to the shipped single-width occupancy at `alphas=(0.2,)` on both RKS and UKS (`max|d| = 0.000e+00`), column order alpha-major then spin (0.000e+00 against that ordering, 1.14e-01 against spin-major), bounded and finite on both spin paths, closed-shell reduction exact, leak-free under the size-consistency test that certified the single-width form, finite nonzero training gradient through a real FULL SCF on H2O and the O atom, and at the tight energy/potential floor on both the RKS and polarized-UKS finite-difference tests. PADDING: `rung35ms_features` joins `_PAD_GRID_EDGE`, but the `(n_alpha, N, nao)` projector stack needs its OWN case with `grid_axis=1, ao_axis=2` -- routing it through `_PAD_AO_ON_GRID` (consumed with `grid_axis=0, ao_axis=1`) returns `(640, 500, 7)` instead of `(3, 640, 13)`, an ~90x element blow-up that becomes an OOM at production grid size. Verified results-neutral: `(3, 4616, 10) -> (3, 7200, 14)` with the real block bit-identical and padded rows finite. ALSO: `_assemble_pretrain_descriptors` gained a WIDTH GATE, because a stale `.npz` written before a descriptor's width changed did not fail -- a 3-column `dm_all` against the now-2-feature `dm_statistics` produced a 6-wide network input where `n_input_features` was 5, which trains without complaint and is wrong; and `xcquinox/pyscf.py` carried two hardcoded `jnp.zeros((n, 3))` fallbacks that would have emitted a 3-wide column against the 2-wide descriptor. `ArchitectureConfig.dm_entropy_intensive` is KEPT as an inert field: it is a frozen-dataclass field carried by the live cluster array's spec files, which must still unpickle.
  **Why:** a descriptor whose gradient is wrong wherever it is evaluated is not a descriptor, it is a constant with noise attached, and it was actively masking the energy/potential inconsistency of the architectures that used it. Recording the impossibility result matters more than the removal: the obvious replacements are all spectral invariants, all of them are constant on the manifold every converged density lives on, and without that argument written down the next attempt reaches for the participation ratio again. The width change is checkpoint-invalidating for the `deep_dm` / `deep_combined` families under `notebooks/checkpoints_v3b`, which is accepted because their gradient was wrong before the change in any case; no architecture in the dfs6311 sweep uses `dm_statistics` -- verified against all 88 live spec files, so the running array is untouched.

- 2026-08-09 -- **Run-directory validator (`cluster/validate_run.py`) and architecture-shape provenance in `pretrain_metadata.json`.** The validator loads every `spec_NNNN.spec` a run actually executes and asserts it against `resolved_config.yaml` and the architecture registry: spec-file completeness against the sweep-axes product under the submit path's own `expand_grid` ordering (a cell's list position IS its array task index), per-spec architecture equality with the one-directional sweep-level polarization override applied, hyperparameters, per-molecule basis and grid level, reference paths inside the configured reference directories, and the named solver block field by field plus `density_fit`/`auxbasis`. Report-only by design: disagreements are listed and the exit status is nonzero, nothing is modified. Run against the live dfs6311 run (88 specs): clean, 16 warnings, all of the legacy-metadata class below. `pretrain_metadata.json` now records `meta_gga` and `n_extra_features` alongside the existing `pretrain_steps`, so a checkpoint's architecture shape can be cross-checked against the spec that loads it; the validator checks the step count under `pretrain_steps`, the key the writer has always emitted, which makes it checkable even on metadata written before the shape keys existed -- confirmed on the live run, where the step count is now actively checked on all eight architectures and matches the config. Missing shape keys are reported as a warning, not a failure, since files written before 2026-08-09 cannot carry them. The end-to-end pretrain tests that guard the writer had never run -- they were gated on a fixture file that was never generated (and whose filename `run_pretrain` does not load in any case); they now run against tiny session-generated data (He, sto-3g, grid 0) produced by the production writer, so the test data's schema tracks the writer by construction.
  **Why:** the 2026-08-06 density-channel diagnosis was slowed by a scoping error -- a conclusion about the run was drawn from a registry default instead of the run's own artifacts. The check that would have caught it in seconds is now a checked-in tool instead of a one-off, and the metadata gap it exposed (a checkpoint whose architecture shape cannot be verified against its consumer) is closed at the writer end.

- 2026-08-10 (MEASUREMENT; constrains re-runs of the meta-GGA sweep cells) -- **Production-scale verification of the corrected V_xc (live run's pretrained checkpoints, 6-311++G(3df,2pd), grid 3, 25 cycles, single-threaded BLAS): the gradient-freeze removal STANDS, and the corrected potential destabilizes the plain meta-GGA SCF on two of eight configurations.** Leg 1 (the alpha-tail regression): the full-SCF training gradient is finite and nonzero at every leaf for deep_mgga_3x16 and deep_rung35_mgga_3x16 on H, Li, LiH and H2O, with |g|max between 3.3e-3 and 5.7e-1 -- nothing at the 2.2e31 scale that motivated the old freeze. Leg 2 (converged-energy shift, corrected vs prior assembly): at or below 0.34 kcal/mol in magnitude on six of eight configurations (deep_rung35_mgga: 1.8e-5 to 0.34), so the energies the shipped cells reported are not materially moved by the missing term; the two larger values (deep_mgga H +0.163, LiH +1.25) belong to the unstable configurations below and sample an oscillating trajectory rather than a converged difference. Leg 3 (convergence profile, energy vs cycle cap): WITH the feature-response term, deep_mgga on H and LiH oscillates under the production mixer -- late-steps 2.2e-1 and 6.7e-2 Ha against 1.1e-10 and 1.2e-8 for the prior assembly, and the prior H run actually converges while the corrected one does not (E at caps 5..25 swings through -0.2747 at cap 15) -- while deep_mgga on Li/H2O settles to ~3e-8 like the prior, and EVERY deep_rung35_mgga configuration is stable (worst late-step 1.6e-5, monotone approach). The root cause is consistent with the recorded tail stiffness of the iso-orbital alpha (max|d alpha/d sigma| ~ 2.2e31 on Li at this basis): the term the fix adds carries that sensitivity into V_xc itself, and plain decaying linear mixing amplifies it on tail-dominated species. DETECTION SIGNAL: the late-step of an energy-vs-cycle-cap profile (5,10,15,20,25). CONSEQUENCE: re-running the deep_mgga sweep cells under the corrected potential without stabilization is expected to inject SCF oscillation noise on tail-dominated species (the H atom is in every atomization reference), while deep_rung35_mgga cells are safe to re-run as-is. REMEDY PATH (not yet implemented, deliberately): damp or precondition the meta-GGA feature-response contribution through the ENERGY (so the potential stays the exact functional derivative -- a gradient freeze is what this project just removed and is not an option), or move the training SCF to DIIS; recorded as the gating item for any meta-GGA cell re-run.
  **Why:** the fix's correctness and its operational cost are different facts, and this measurement separates them: the potential is now the true functional derivative (leg 1, and the FD tests at commit time), the shipped energies were not materially wrong (leg 2), and the one real cost is SCF stiffness on two tail-dominated configurations (leg 3) -- which is a mixing problem with a named detection signal, not a reason to reintroduce a wrong derivative.

- 2026-08-10 -- **v4 sweep defined (`dfs_step7.dfs6311_grid3_v4.yaml`): corrected-V_xc re-run of the rung-3.5 families only, 33 cells.** The v3 array was stopped beyond spec 0047: every completed cell predates the V_xc correction, and the tail additionally straddled a mid-flight library sync (task 47 imported the old library, tasks 48/49 the corrected one) plus a pretrain refresh, leaving the not-yet-run cells with no clean provenance class. The v3 record therefore closes as the internally consistent pre-correction block, specs 0000-0047. v4 re-runs ONLY what the correction affects and what is stable under it: `deep_rung35_3x16`, `deep_rung35_attn_3x16`, `deep_rung35_mgga_3x16`. The GGA/cusp families are excluded because their completed v3 cells are already correct (grid-local descriptors; the correction never touched their potential), and the plain meta-GGA families are excluded pending SCF stabilization (the corrected potential oscillates on tail-dominated species) and the (s, alpha)-mesh pretrain (the SCAN clone scores 42.65 kcal/mol pretrained-only against SCAN's 4.45 on the same 164 reactions). Everything else -- subsets, solver, hyperparameters, reference data, basis, grid, orientation lock -- is identical to v3, and `loss_vxc` deliberately keeps its frozen-feature definition (DEFERRED_WORK item 10) so the corrected SCF potential is the SINGLE variable of the A/B. Configuration verified by execution before submission: all three architectures descend under the production loss with finite live gradients (open-shell included), the 3-cycle corrected trajectory contracts where the prior one does, the fresh pretrain stage writes correct provenance keys per architecture, the parsed config differs from v3 in exactly the architecture axis and output root, and the train wall is 48 h with a ~256 GB partition requirement -- v3's own resolved config ran at 48 h and its measured attention-cell durations reach 42.14 h, so the 24 h default the YAML inherited would have wall-killed the large-subset attention cells. The shared pretrain-data npz will be regenerated in place by the datagen stage (the 2026-07-28 file predates the `rung35ms_all` column the staleness check now requires); the regeneration is value-identical for every column this sweep consumes.
  **Why:** the re-sweep exists to measure the correction's effect on trained outcomes against the pre-correction control block, so it must change exactly one thing. Excluding the families whose cells are either already correct or known-unstable keeps the 33 node-days spent where they answer a question.

- 2026-08-10 -- **(s, alpha) parameter-space pretrain mesh shipped, and the v4 sweep widened to the full descriptor-ablation grid (7 architectures, 77 cells) with the meta-GGA families back in.** The mesh closes the measured SCAN-clone failure: SCAN's enhancement factors are 3-D in (r_s, s, alpha) and the atomic grids leave the alpha axis underdetermined -- the pretrained-only meta-GGA net scored 42.65 kcal/mol on the held-out reactions against SCAN's own 4.45 on the same set. `pretrain_data_gen` now evaluates SCAN's F_x/F_c on a regular (r_s, s, alpha) mesh (7 x 8 x 10 nodes; each node realized as a physical (rho, sigma, tau) triple and pushed through the SAME libxc calls and the SAME clipped ratio-minus-one convention as the atomic path, with alpha recomputed through the shared `compute_alpha` so a divergence from the SCF descriptor would surface in the mesh itself), stored under separate `*_mesh` npz keys with a stated MESH_WEIGHT_FRACTION = 0.3 share of the quadrature weight recorded in the manifest, and guarded by the staleness check so pre-mesh files regenerate. `run_pretrain` appends the mesh ONLY for a meta-GGA arch whose descriptor set is exactly `(metagga,)` -- a mesh node has no geometry, so it cannot define cusp or rung-3.5 columns, and `deep_rung35_mgga`'s pretrain deliberately stays atoms-only with that caveat carried on the sweep config. The checkpoint records `pretrain_mesh` and the run validator cross-checks it against the registry-derived expectation. WEIGHTING DEFECT FOUND AND FIXED THE SAME DAY: the first shipped form normalized the mesh's share of the QUADRATURE weights to 0.3, but the production `integration` loss weighting multiplies `|rho*eps_LDA|` on top, and the mesh's synthesized densities (up to rho ~ 2.4e2 a.u. at r_s = 0.1) then took an EFFECTIVE 0.99997 of the loss weight -- the physical atoms carried ~3e-5, the atomic-manifold F_c fit degraded 12.6x (weighted RMS 0.246, max at the 2.0 ceiling), and the first efficacy measurement had silently run at the `unweighted` default, masking all of it. The fix computes the integration weights on the atomic block alone and appends a FLAT per-channel mesh block normalized so the mesh's share of each channel's total loss weight is exactly MESH_WEIGHT_FRACTION by construction; a regression test asserts the post-weighting share and flatness (the earlier test asserted only the quadrature ratio, which is how the defect shipped). MEASURED EFFICACY under the production `integration` weighting, after the fix (H+O/def2-svp/grid-0 data, 600-step pretrains, 378 off-manifold probe nodes at geometric midpoints of the mesh grids, reported with an in-support split against the atomic rows' exchange-weighted 1-99% box): off-manifold F_c error falls from 1.170 max / 0.237 mean without the mesh to 0.212 / 0.040 with it (5.9x on the mean); the IN-SUPPORT slice -- the region the SCF-visited densities occupy -- improves 0.116 -> 0.047; F_x improves 0.118 / 0.027 -> 0.032 / 0.007; the cost on the atomic training manifold is a weighted F_c RMS of 0.033 against 0.020 without the mesh, the deliberate price of the 30% share. Non-mesh architectures are BYTE-IDENTICAL with and without the mesh keys present. GATE MEASUREMENT for re-including the meta-GGA families: training and holdout eval both run full_3, so the 25-cycle oscillation recorded 2026-08-10 is never entered by the sweep; the previously unmeasured 3-cycle corrected trajectory for `deep_mgga_3x16` is finite on H/Li/LiH/H2O from both a seed and a mesh-pretrained initialization. GATE (absolute-first, measured at def2-svp/grid 1): last step at or below 0.05 kcal/mol with non-increasing steps, or no worse than the prior assembly the completed v3 cells trained through; trajectories still moving at cycle 3 are extended in horizon rather than judged there. Seed init: settled on all four species. Mesh init: settled on H/Li/LiH; H2O carries a cycle-2 transient (steps 0.104 -> 0.194 kcal/mol at 3 cycles) that a 6-cycle horizon resolves -- the steps turn over and decay 0.194 -> 0.097 -> 0.062 -> 0.042, under the 0.05 threshold by cycle 6, with the corrected ENERGY descending monotonically the whole way while the prior assembly bounces and settles fast onto its self-inconsistent fixed point. Cycle-cap 3 was added to the production verification job so the 3-cycle point is confirmed at production scale. The v4 sweep is now `deep_mgga`, `deep_mgga_attn` (SCAN + mesh pretrain), `deep_rung35only`, `deep_rung35`, `deep_rung35_attn`, `deep_rung35ms` (PBE-form pretrain), and `deep_rung35_mgga` (SCAN, no mesh) -- the ablation chains for attention, cusp, multi-width rung-3.5, metagga stacking, and rung-3 vs rung-3.5, with v3's valid GGA rows as the rung-2 baseline. Pretrain-target policy recorded on the config: each arch pretrains to the best functional its inputs can EXACTLY represent (SCAN is multivalued at fixed (rho, sigma), so the GGA-based rung-3.5 archs keep PBE -- faithful at <= 0.013, SCF-safe, and the DFS paper's own protocol).
  **Why:** the meta-GGA rows' failure was an initialization failure, not a training failure -- in-sample they fit tighter than the GGA family while the held-out numbers collapsed -- and the mesh attacks that root cause with the DFS SI's own remedy (regular-grid augmentation in parameter space). Re-including the meta-GGA families without the stabilization work is justified by measurement, not hope: the sweep never leaves the 3-cycle regime, and at 3 cycles the corrected potential is better-behaved than the one v3 already trained through.

- 2026-08-10 (RESULTS-AFFECTING for the interpretation of every completed run) -- **A source-level and executed review of the entire training workflow found one configuration defect and confirmed the derivative chain end to end.** THE DEFECT: `_config_to_raw_dict` (the serializer that writes `resolved_config.yaml`) omitted `ae_as_reactions`, and the preflight re-reads that file before building specs, so the flag never reached spec assembly -- every production run (v2, v3, v3_full25) trained the atomization channel in the FIXED-ANCHOR relative form its source YAML had turned off. Confirmed on the v3 artifacts: `resolved_config.yaml` lacks the key, `aux_log.pkl` carries 21 live `ae:<name>` groups where the reaction form emits none, and `spec_0010` holds 3 BH76 reactions where the reaction form yields 24. The serializer is fixed, a round-trip test now iterates `dataclasses.fields(GridConfig)` so a dropped field can never ship again, and the three documents that described the reaction form as live (LOSS_PRIMER twice, the 2026-06-22 entry's premise) are corrected. DECISION for v4: keep `ae_as_reactions: true`, now real -- the reaction form is the DFS-faithful one (dpyscf scores atomization against the network's own atom energies) and the fixed-anchor form is the documented source of the Na2 blow-up; the cost, recorded on the sweep config, is that the rung-3.5 rows' cross-run A/B against v3 carries the corrected potential AND the AE-channel form jointly. CONFIRMED BY EXECUTION, on the production device (CPU): the complete v4 loss composition -- tail-weighted 3-cycle unroll, density fitting, orientation lock, polarized correlation, per-molecule scheme, fixed channel weights -- matches a central finite difference at 2.0e-8..3.0e-8 over random parameter directions, with the de-fused per-molecule gradient equal to the fused one at machine precision; the tail loss's zero-weight first cycle still carries gradient through the recursion (severing it moves the gradient by 3e-3 relative); `loss_vxc` is an exact derivative of its own frozen-feature definition with bit-zero leakage in either direction; GradNorm is provably inert under `per_molecule`; and the orientation lock enters the loss and its finite difference through the same biased `h_core`, so it cannot bias gradients asymmetrically. An apparent 1.1e-3 AD-vs-FD gap on the density channel was traced to the workstation's CUDA backend and retracted -- the CPU value is 1.9e-9 -- but the finding stands that the density channel is not backend-stable at the ~1e-4 level, so GPU training would need its own verification (the sweep pins `device: cpu`). PARITY-TABLE ADDITIONS from reading the vendored dpyscf source directly (LOSS_PRIMER Sec. 8 now carries all of these): the SCF convergence freeze (ours; DFS runs all 25 cycles unconditionally; fires on 22.9% of v3 molecule-instances, a theta-dependent branch); DFS's per-step randomized SCF initialization and half-normal V_xc noise versus our deterministic PBE start and SYM_BREAK shift; the mixer's one-step index offset against the paper's own code (we match the SI equation, first mix alpha = 1.3; their code no-ops step 0, first effective alpha 0.6); the pretrain mesh's dimensionality, scope and weighting relative to the SI's exchange-only equal-weight (s, alpha) grid; and the SI's supervised H/Li atomic densities, which this pipeline does not supervise. ALSO RESTATED: DEFERRED item 10's `loss_vxc` share -- the earlier 0.1-3.6% figure was the median over all updates including the ~25% where the channel is zero; over vxc-active updates the per-spec median reaches 48.15% on the `subset_size = 1` cells, so the channel is a first-order term at the ablation curves' leftmost points (the deferral stands on definition-consistency across the A/B, not on smallness). And `evaluation.py` now forwards the spec's DF auxbasis like the held-out path, closing a silent train/eval fitting-basis divergence that a configured auxbasis would have triggered.
  **Why:** the pre-submission question was whether the training workflow computes the derivatives it claims and deviates from the Letter only where documented. The derivatives held under attack; the parity story did not -- the most consequential gap was not physics but plumbing, a silently dropped config field that inverted a documented methodology choice across three run generations. The fix class (round-trip every config field, assert the realized loss composition) is cheap; the lesson -- the artifact a run re-reads, not the file the user wrote, is the configuration -- is the same one the validator was built on.

- 2026-08-10 -- **Third sweep arm defined: the meta-GGA stacking completions (`dfs6311_grid3_v4mgga2`, 22 cells), with two new registry architectures.** `deep_cusp_mgga_3x16` (cusp + metagga) isolates whether the cusp descriptor helps at the meta-GGA rung -- the deep_cusp-vs-deep chain lifted to rung 3 -- and `deep_rung35ms_mgga_3x16` (cusp + multi-width rung-3.5 + metagga) is the multi-width A/B against `deep_rung35_mgga` at the same rung, and the first architecture that threads the multishell projector tensor AND the metagga `ao_grid_deriv` through one V_xc. Both joined the per-family derivative-consistency battery and pass its full set on first run (elementwise V_xc = sym(dE/dP) with the feature-response term shown load-bearing, training gradient vs central finite difference for the energy AND density losses, calibrated energy/density descent). Both pretrain to SCAN WITHOUT the (s, alpha) mesh -- a geometry-free mesh node cannot define cusp or projection columns -- so their initializations carry the documented underdetermined-alpha caveat `deep_rung35_mgga` already carries. The arm submits on long-96core after the first meta-GGA arm completes (same queue budget; the shared pretrain npz is current by then, so its datagen skips). Together the three arms cover the descriptor stacks at both rungs: {none, cusp, cusp+rung35, cusp+rung35ms} x {GGA, meta-GGA}, less the never-built bare metagga+multishell-without-cusp cell.
  **Why:** the ablation question is "what descriptors help", and without these two cells the cusp and multi-width effects are measurable only at the GGA rung -- any rung-3 claim about them would be extrapolation. Registering the caveated SCAN clone rather than waiting for a geometry-aware mesh keeps the arm comparable to arm 1's stacking cell, which carries the same initialization.

- 2026-08-10 (production measurement, closing the last verification gap) -- **The four-architecture verification job confirms the 3-cycle regime stable at production scale for every architecture in the v4 arms.** All sixteen leg-1 training gradients finite and nonzero, including the first production-scale measurement of `deep_rung35_3x16` and `deep_rung35_attn_3x16` (|g|max 0.05-1.2), whose corrected-potential behaviour had rested on the linearity argument. The new cycle-cap-3 leg -- the regime the sweeps train AND eval in -- is smooth and monotone for all four architectures under both potentials; the deep_mgga oscillation reproduces only at caps 15-25 (H swings through -0.2747 at cap 15), confirming at scale that it is quarantined to the full_25 follow-up. Corrected-vs-prior converged shifts: rung-3.5 family -0.003 to -0.28 kcal/mol with one larger, smooth-trajectory outlier (`deep_rung35_attn`/Li, -0.86 kcal/mol -- a genuine lower fixed point, not noise). Measured on the v3 (non-mesh) checkpoints; the v4 arms' mesh-fixed meta-GGA initializations are better-conditioned than the tested ones.
  **Why:** the sweeps were submitted on the strength of local trajectory gates and an unmeasured pure-rung-3.5 assumption; this measurement replaces both with production-scale evidence before any array cell runs.

- 2026-08-12 -- **v4 figure pipeline unblocked: full arm tokens in the pull-and-plot wrapper; `ARCH_ORDER` completed for the v4 architectures.** The per-arm suite calls in `notebooks/analysis/pull_and_plot_v4.sh` passed prefix-stripped `--bases` tokens (`v4`), which the suite resolver joins literally as `<results_root>/dfs_step7/<basis>/runs` -- every per-arm suite died in FileNotFoundError behind the wrapper's warning guard while the merged view built normally; the wrapper now passes the full arm names. `notebooks/analysis/arch_style.py` gained the three v4 architectures still absent from `ARCH_ORDER` (`deep_rung35ms_3x16`, `deep_cusp_mgga_3x16`, `deep_rung35ms_mgga_3x16`) with palette entries in the module's tab20b scheme; the suite fails loud on any eval'd architecture outside that tuple, so the GGA arm's rung-3.5-multishell block would have killed its suite on arrival. A third defect surfaced while checking the additions: the registry-derived rung classifier tested `"rung35" in names` by exact set membership, but the multishell descriptor is registered as `rung35_multishell`, so both multishell architectures landed on the wrong Jacob's-ladder rung (`deep_rung35ms_3x16` as GGA, `deep_rung35ms_mgga_3x16` as plain meta-GGA) -- every rung-banded figure would have grouped and labeled them under the wrong rung, splitting the `deep_rung35ms_mgga`-vs-`deep_rung35_mgga` A/B across two bands. Detection is now by name prefix; verified over all 31 registered architectures (exactly the two multishell entries move, base names and `_3x16` twins agree everywhere). Regression tests pin the wrapper token form, the full 11-architecture campaign roster with distinct colors, and per-architecture rung placement; the registry cross-check test previously recomputed the classifier's own predicate (circular) and now anchors on explicit expected rungs. Rendering-layer only; no results change.
  **Why:** the arm suites are the primary view of the live campaign, and all three defects were silent-until-later -- one masked by the wrapper's `|| echo` guard, the others waiting for the multishell architecture blocks to finish training.

- 2026-08-12 -- **Merged cross-arm view promoted to the primary v4 figure product: full figure families, val-best variant, and PBE + SCAN reference lines on one frame.** The per-arm figure directories split the cross-architecture comparison and carried the reference functionals only as footnote text on the merged set. The merged view now renders every figure family (`build_density_energy_figures` joined `build_all` in the wrapper's merged step) in final-step and validation-best variants; `plot_mae_vs_subset` draws the dashed-PBE and dotted-SCAN full-pool reference lines on the held-out panel (SCAN gated by the existing coverage floor), orders architectures by Jacob's-ladder rung, and distinguishes rungs by linestyle; a new `ablation_arch_subset_heatmap_vs_pbe` grid shows the per-cell NN/PBE MAE ratio on a diverging scale about 1.0 (per-cell PBE computed on exactly the reactions the NN was scored on). SCAN resolution on the merged view required two pieces of plumbing: `merge_v4_arms.build_view` now propagates `resolved_config.yaml` and `scan_pool_*.json` from the arm runs into the view (basis-label resolution previously degraded to "unknown" there, which is why no merged figure ever showed SCAN), and the wrapper seeds each arm's newest run dir from the canonical local cache mirror `~/Documents/Research/xcquinox-results/scan_pool_6311ppg3df2pd_g3/`. A latent inconsistency was closed in passing: `build_all` had called `scan_pool_baseline` without forwarding `eval_subdir`. Rendering-layer only; no results change.
  **Why:** the campaign's headline question -- which architectures beat the reference functionals, where -- needs every arm, PBE, and SCAN in a single frame; footnote-only baselines and per-arm directory splits made that comparison manual.

- 2026-08-12 -- **PHYSICAL ARTIFACT: the c2 grid-drift class resurfaced across the v4 arms -- the GGA arm's eval carries a drifted c2 PBE reference energy.** The two live arms disagree on exactly one species: E_pbe(c2) = -75.816711949 Ha in the meta-GGA arm (agreeing exactly with the trusted post-repair v3 run) vs -75.757329256 Ha in the GGA arm (run_20260810T202813Z) -- a 37.26 kcal/mol drift, against <= 3.6e-12 Ha spread within each arm. The +0.059 Ha sign and the species are the signature of the previously closed c2 pathology (c2 is the one pool species whose plain PBE kernel does not converge; the escalation path once stored intermediates pruned on a non-converged density, and deletion without the intermediates re-drifts -- see `hpcjobs/dfs6311_c2_ref_probe.py`). **Detection signal, now codified:** cross-spec PBE-map disagreement in the figure layer -- `_first_pbe_energies` pools every spec's per_molecule.json, and any species spreading beyond 1e-4 Ha (SCF noise is ~2.5e-6) is EXCLUDED from the reference baselines with a printed warning naming the species and values; `scan_pool_baseline` restricts both legs to the PBE-computable pool so the drawn PBE and SCAN lines always average the same reactions. The merged cross-arm view therefore draws deterministic, c2-free reference lines regardless of arm ordering (previously it silently inherited whichever arm's spec sorted first: combined 11.777 vs 11.950 kcal/mol depending on source). Per-arm figures are arm-self-consistent and unaffected. NN energies are untouched (the drift is in the PBE comparison column only); no current cell's beats-PBE verdict flips (nearest cell sits 0.58 kcal/mol from the affected band). **Remedy (deferred to arm drain; the array is mid-flight):** regenerate the GGA arm's c2 PBE eval reference with the mandatory-intermediates-deletion procedure and re-run the affected specs' held-out evals, then the exclusion warning disappears on its own; logged in `DEFERRED_WORK.md`.
  **Why:** a silent 37 kcal/mol reference inconsistency between arms would make every cross-arm PBE comparison depend on directory iteration order; excluding the species loudly is the only choice that neither hides the artifact nor lets either candidate value contaminate the shared baseline.

- 2026-08-12 -- **Headline combined-ED figures now carry the SCAN comparator.** `build_density_energy_figures` computed the SCAN legs only for the per-channel 3x3 path; the headline `combined_ed_by_cell` calls (WTMAD-2 and MAE energy legs) omitted `e_scan`/`d_scan`, so `ablation_combined_energy_density*` never drew `ed_scan` even with both caches present (found while verifying the density-cache delivery: the density per-arch figure gained its SCAN line, the ED figure did not). The WTMAD-2 leg reuses `wtmad2_scan_baseline`; a new `scan_reaction_mae_baseline` supplies the like-for-like MAE-leg twin; both are coverage-gated exactly as in the channel path, and the ED CSVs gained the SCAN columns (blank when the cache is absent). The DFS-units headline legs (`wtmad2_eps_gamma_dfs`/`_fit`) carry the same comparator with the density leg re-anchored on the Eq. 20 eps channel, so a given cell's `ED_scan` now agrees between `ablation_combined_energy_density.csv` and the 3x3 DFS-units CSV (previously blank in one, populated in the other). Consistency items closed while threading: `wtmad2_scan_baseline` counts its reference set over the PBE-computable rows -- the set its docstring claims and the MAE twin always used; previously rows unscored by both PBE and SCAN could push the WTMAD-2 leg under the 90% coverage floor while the MAE leg stayed above it. Drawn-but-partial comparators carry their counts in the panel labels (`scan_suffix`, `", E u/r D u/r"`), the overview footer states whether panel F draws the comparator instead of unconditionally claiming "no SCAN lines" (`_overview_provenance`), the ED/eps provenance lines name the comparator coverage, and a suite pass reports the SCAN console state exactly once (energy baseline in `build_all`, density anchor in `build_density_energy_figures`). Rendering-layer only; no results change.
  **Why:** the combined ED is the Letter-comparable headline metric; a SCAN line on the density and per-channel figures but not on the headline ED would read as SCAN lacking ED coverage rather than as a threading gap.

- 2026-08-12 -- **deep_attn low-ss regression in v4 diagnosed: not an execution defect -- the two v3->v4 corrections removed the implicit regularizers the v3 attention advantage depended on.** v3 `deep_attn` led the GGA family from subset_size 2 (val-best combined 6.50-7.04 kcal/mol at ss 2-5, vs deep's 10.1-11.1, advantage persisting to ss 26); v4 `deep_attn` collapsed at exactly those cells (22.25 / 12.13 at ss 2/3) while v4 `deep` improved at every low ss. Execution ruled out on three measurements: v4 attn training descends smoothly to ~1e-6 median loss -- 10-30x BELOW v3 attn's ~1e-4 plateau (a broken path does not minimize better); no attention-path code changed between the runs (the one arch-adjacent commit in the window touched dm_statistics/rung35_multishell, which deep_attn does not use); and v4 attn ss1 improved over v3 (8.95 vs 13.86). Mechanism, from the training artifacts: (i) the corrected V_xc removed a gradient-energy inconsistency whose magnitude is architecture-dependent -- largest for attention nets through the attention weights' feature response -- and which had acted as a training noise floor; (ii) the reaction-form AE, enabled for the first time by the serializer fix (v3 trained fixed-anchor despite its YAML), drains the AE channel at tiny subsets: the AE share of the summed channel losses over the final 25 training epochs (from the runs' per-update `aux_log.pkl`) falls from 71.2%/74.3% (v3 attn, ss2/ss3) to 9.0%/2.8% in v4, removing the absolute-anchor calibration pressure while the dormant V_xc monitor's share grows to 70.4%/86.8%. With both implicit regularizers gone, the attention capacity fits 2-3 training molecules essentially exactly and generalization collapses; deep's 3x16-without-attention cannot overfit that hard and gained from both corrections. Decision gate: if v4 attn has not recovered to ~v3 level (~6.5 val-best combined) by ss >= 7 (those cells are in flight), a 2-cell controlled A/B (deep_attn ss2 with `ae_as_reactions: false` at the corrected V_xc, additive YAML, submitted only after the running array drains) separates the two mechanisms.
  **Why:** the regression pattern-matched a broken attention path; the recorded evidence shows it is a consequence of two deliberate physics/methodology corrections, names the two mechanisms and their signatures, and defines the observable that would overturn the interpretation -- so the question is settled by data already queued rather than re-litigated.

- 2026-08-13 -- **Strict-holdout validity repairs: species-naming leak closed, validation twins excluded, cell metrics deduplicated, beats-PBE verdicts cell-matched, density anchors made pull-stable.** Four defects in the held-out evaluation/figure chain, each confirmed by executing independent recomputations against the pulled v4 artifacts, then fixed test-first:
  (i) LEAK: the cluster-side strict held-out filter matches species by (case-folded) NAME, but training names are ASE Hill formulas from the DFS pool builder (`CHN`, `H3N`, `HO`, `CH2`) while the benchmark pools name the same molecules GMTKN55-style (`hcn`, `nh3`, `oh`, `ch2-trip`) -- so trained molecules' reactions stayed in the reported "held-out" rows (`w411_hcn_atomization` in every cell whose subset trains `CHN`, with |err_nn| 0.08-5.6 kcal/mol vs PBE 13.7 on the validation-best channel: memorization, not generalization). FIX: `xcquinox/alec/species_matching.py` matches species identity on (element composition, charge, spin), with composition-degenerate isomers resolved against the trained geometry from the pool builder's own G2/97 trajectory (rotation/translation/permutation-invariant per-element-pair distance signatures; `hcn` vs `hnc`, `ch2-trip` vs `ch2-sing`, and `c2h2` vs `ch2c` all separate correctly -- ambiguity is assessed over ALL composition matches BEFORE name-visible candidates are removed, since dropping the name-visible isomer first leaves its twin as a false lone match). Wired into `eval_holdout.held_out_filter_names_with_aliases` (cluster deploy deferred to array drain; the running array live-imports the tree) and applied by the figure collectors on read (122 reaction + 39 density rows dropped across the 25 evaluated v4 cells on the merged view; per-spec counts printed).
  (ii) VALIDATION TWINS: the pool lists four BH76 barriers twice under permuted-reactant names; the name-hashed val/test split put one copy per slice, so validation-best selection saw four reported test barriers. FIX: the split hashes the order-invariant physical identity (`reaction_identity_key`; twins now land on one side), the eval prefers the RECORDED `validation/val_reactions.json` over recomputation (re-evals of existing runs keep their historical partition) and excludes by identity, and the figure layer drops the four test-side twins on read (96 rows over the 25 evaluated specs).
  (iii) DEDUP: cell WTMAD-2/MAE double-counted the pool's four duplicate-name entries while the PBE baselines deduplicated (0.8-4.8% per-cell WTMAD-2 shift on the pre-repair rows); cell metrics and the CSV n_reactions counts now dedup by name within each cell.
  (iv) ANCHOR FAIRNESS + DRIFT: each cell scores a training-subset-dependent reaction/species subset (114-157 deduped names after repairs) but was graded against ONE pooled-union PBE anchor -- on the WTMAD-2 ED legs every evaluated cell's pooled anchor sat above its cell-matched value (25/25; 6/25 on the MAE leg), overstating the beats-PBE margins; and the pooled density anchor averaged the c2 species' two cross-arm-inconsistent PBE references with pull-coverage weights, moving gamma between renders (36788.6 -> 36890.6) with no data change. FIX: beats-PBE verdicts are judged against cell-matched PBE reductions (PBE over exactly the cell's scored set; drawn as per-cell ticks beside the pooled dashed line, `ED_pbe_cell_kcalmol` in the CSVs, convention + anchor range stated in the footers), and species with cross-spec-inconsistent PBE density references (>5% relative spread; c2 at 11x) leave the density rows entirely, so no anchor moves with pull coverage.
  Effect on the merged val-best view (combined WTMAD-2): every cell improves 4-24% on the repaired set with the PBE anchor moving in the same direction (8.781 -> 8.134; gamma 36890.6 -> 35391.6; ED_scan 6.353 -> 5.826); deep_attn ss7 4.83 vs cell anchor 7.72 (the ss-7 recovery stands, margin wider); deep_mgga ss5 3.91; three beats-PBE verdicts flip to False under the cell-matched anchors (deep_3x16 ss4/ss12/ss26 -- ss26: NN combined ED 6.93 vs its own-rows anchor 5.76, where the old pooled line 8.78 read as a win).
  The SCAN comparator carries the same misgrading in the opposite direction and is closed the same way: SCAN reduced over each cell's surviving rows is far BELOW its pooled value (combined WTMAD-2 5.97 on the pre-repair union, 5.28 on the surviving 161-name union, vs cell-matched 5.10 at ss1 down to 2.70 at ss26 -- the reactions the strict filter removes are disproportionately SCAN's worst cases, mean |err| 5.8-7.8 removed vs 3.9-4.3 kept), so pooled-only beats-SCAN verdicts flattered every cell. Per-cell SCAN reductions (wtmad2_scan_by_cell / scan_reaction_mae_by_cell / scan_density_by_cell, coverage-gated per cell at the standing 90% floor) now back the beats_scan verdicts (`ED_scan_cell_kcalmol` in the CSVs) and draw as grey ticks beside the black PBE ticks, with the pooled lines relabeled. Re-graded result: exactly one of 25 cells beats SCAN on the combined metric (deep_mgga ss5, 4.74 vs 4.82) where the pooled comparison had claimed five. The green beats marker is additionally RUNG-REFERENCED: each architecture is marked only when it beats its own-rung nonempirical reference's cell-matched anchor -- PBE for pure-GGA architectures, SCAN for meta-GGA and rung-3.5 families (the rung-3.5 assignment is a stated convention: nonlocal DM information but no tau leaves them with no same-rung reference, and SCAN is the conservative choice); a cell whose reference anchor is withdrawn stays unmarked rather than falling back to a pooled line (the arch-aggregate summary figure, which has no cells, grades against the full-pool reductions). The CSVs keep both verdict columns (beats_pbe, beats_scan) regardless of which one the marker shows.
  **Why (rung marker):** a beyond-GGA architecture beating PBE is expected from its extra inputs alone; the marker's claim must be improvement over the strongest nonempirical functional of comparable information content, or it reads as an achievement it is not.
  **Why (SCAN leg):** a comparator graded on a different reaction set than the cells is the same defect whether it flatters PBE or the network; the SCAN claims are the paper's strongest, so they must rest on same-set reductions. Documented in `README_density_figures.md` (repairs + CSV schema) and `HOLDOUT_SET.md` (twin caveat); cluster-side re-eval at drain is DEFERRED_WORK #17.
  **Why:** the held-out claim is the paper's central evidence; rows that were trained on, seen by model selection, double-counted, or graded against a mismatched anchor are not held-out evidence, and every repair moves the comparison toward the stricter reading -- the surviving story (NN beats PBE on the combined metric at nearly every cell, meta-GGA density dominance, attention's small-data collapse and ss-7 recovery) now rests on rows that are verifiably disjoint from training and selection.

- 2026-08-13 -- **Held-out redefined: exclusion by VERBATIM supervised reaction, not species membership.** Supersedes the reaction-side of the same-day strict-holdout repair (the density-side species exclusion and the c2 consistency guard stand). A model's reported test slice now excludes exactly (a) its verbatim supervised reactions -- the reaction-form training points recorded per spec in `loss_kwargs["bh76_reactions"]`: the AE-as-reactions (whose `w411_*_atomization` pool twins leave under cross-vocabulary identity; the three trained OVERALL barrier-pair reactions have no to-TS pool twin, and the FLi/HLi/Na2 atomizations have no pool twin at all) -- and (b) the recorded validation slice (identity-level, unchanged). A reaction merely CONTAINING a trained molecule is a generalization target and stays. Identities are canonical composition/charge/spin keys with geometry classes clustering the pool's case twins (O/o, NH3/nh3) while separating true isomers (hcn/hnc, verified against the pool builder's G2/97 geometries); coefficient magnitudes participate, so differing stoichiometries never match. Cluster eval: `trained_reaction_exclusion` + identity drops in `_finalize_holdout_outputs` (species overlap remains as the per-row `in_sample_overlap` annotation and per-molecule flag; `test_set.csv` schema unchanged, the drop column now counts verbatim drops). Figure layer: per-spec test slices are RECONSTRUCTED from the pulled per-species energies (`E_total_nn`/`E_pbe` in `per_molecule.json`, all 214 pool species, every checkpoint channel) through `eval_holdout.per_reaction_errors` -- the cluster's own reaction math -- with the same identity exclusions, so already-evaluated cells reach parity with post-deployment cluster evals without re-running anything; pulls predating the energy columns fall back to the cluster-written rows under the previous repairs. Measured on the merged validation-best view: per-cell test sets 161-163 rows at ss1-7 and 147 at ss26 (previously 161 shrinking to 127), exclusions per spec = its verbatim twins (1 at ss1, 18 at ss26) plus the ~52-entry validation slice; `w411_c2_atomization` sits in the validation slice, which is why no c2 test row ever existed. Headline shift: deep_3x16 ss26's combined 2-subset WTMAD-2 is 6.98 vs same-rows PBE 8.67 -- the previous ss26 "loss" (5.57 vs 4.42) was measured on the species-filtered remainder, a maximally-training-dissimilar barrier subset that the verbatim definition no longer treats as the test set.
  **Why:** held-out means not-verbatim-supervised. Excluding every reaction that shares a molecule with training conflates transferability -- the property being claimed -- with leakage, shrinks the test set as training grows, and turns cross-subset-size comparison into a moving target whose remainder hardens by construction; the verbatim definition fixes the target of the claim and matches the Letter's own evaluation practice. The validation slice stays excluded because early stopping selected on it, and trained species' DENSITIES stay excluded from the held-out density means because each is itself a training target of the 20x density loss term.

- 2026-08-13 -- **Refinalization tooling for stale-rule held-out artifacts.** `xcquinox/alec/refinalize_verbatim.py` (+ `hpcjobs/refinalize_verbatim_holdout.sbatch`) rewrites completed specs' `per_reaction.json`/`test_set.csv` under the verbatim hold-out rule from the per-species energies already in `per_molecule.json` -- no SCF, one-time `*.pre_verbatim.*` backups, idempotent (the run report doubles as the stale-spec list). `notebooks/analysis/verify_holdout_parity.py` diffs cluster-written rows against the figure layer's reconstruction (verdicts: parity / stale-rule / value-mismatch); on the current pulls all 50 spec-channels report stale-rule with zero value mismatches, the set differences decomposing exactly into the four name-hidden validation twins plus the old rule's species-overlap drops. No local results change (the figures already use the reconstruction); this step makes the cluster artifacts agree.
  **Why:** the per-species energies are rule-independent and correct; recomputing only the reaction selection brings every pre-deployment spec to byte-parity with post-deployment evals at negligible cost, and the backups preserve the species-strict artifacts for provenance.

## Phase 37 -- Per-rung SCF seeding groundwork: seed supply layer, config plumbing, replay-drift fix (2026-08-14)

- 2026-08-14 `cb75f7c88`, `d901aa87a` -- rung taxonomy lifted into the library (`rungs.py`: registry-derived `rung_of` + the `seed_xc_for_arch` policy predicate) and the seed-source config plumbing end to end (`SolverConfig.seed_source`/`seed_cache_dir` with metadata/round-trip/validation coverage; `inputs.seed_xc` defaulting `"pbe"` so pending arms cannot silently convert on resubmit; the `eval_coldstart` root flag). Scaffolding only -- no consumer yet, byte-identical behavior everywhere. **Why:** the density-footing asymmetry diagnosis (every rung's truncated SCF seeds from converged PBE while SCAN comparisons use converged SCAN) calls for per-rung baseline seeding; the Letter's own code preps per-campaign converged PBE-or-SCAN baselines, so the campaign switch is the replicated design. The predicate lives in the library so spec building, run validation, and the figure layer agree by construction.
- 2026-08-14 -- **dm_seed supply layer.** `precompute_fixed_density_data` now populates `mol_data["dm_seed"]`: an ALIAS of `dm_pbe` for `seed_source="pbe"` (identity-equal, so the default protocol is unchanged); a converged SCAN dm loaded from the seed cache (`run_scf_with_cache(xc="scan")` intermediates) for `"scan"`, validated by an overlap-matrix fingerprint against the live molecule (filename-only cache identity passes shape checks for isomers -- `allclose(S)` does not); the functional-free minao superposition guess for `"minao"`. The seed never passes through the grid-owning kernel, so the integration grid is identical across seed choices (the c2-class grid-identity rule). Generation on a cache miss is double-gated (training-side call sites only AND `XCQUINOX_SEED_ALLOW_GENERATE=1`, exported only by cluster task scripts); the validation slice asserts pool-cache coverage before training starts. Non-pbe seeds require `mode=FULL` (ONESHOT would silently ignore the seed; FIXED_J would pin the PBE-density Coulomb matrix against a non-PBE D0 -- a mixed-footing Fock). **Why:** supplies the per-rung baseline D0 with the seed identity (basis, grid level, DF, orientation lock, xc) explicit at every step; the lock is part of the cache identity because an unlocked SCAN seed would re-enter the degenerate-component artifact through D0 for the 2-Pi radicals.
- 2026-08-14 -- **The SCF consumes the seed: D0 = dm_seed (per-rung seeding live).** The manual solver's RKS and UKS entry points now start the truncated SCF from `mol_data["dm_seed"]` instead of `dm_pbe`; the pyscfad backend rejects non-PBE seeds outright (it prunes its internal grid on the seed density, which would break the grid-identity guarantees; it is unreachable in production). With the default `"pbe"` seed the two keys are the same array object, so every existing spec, checkpoint, and eval is unchanged -- pinned by a single-record regression in which swapping ONLY the dm_seed slot (dm_pbe and every other input held as the same objects) changes the 3-cycle trajectory, while replacing the alias with an equal-valued copy reproduces it exactly. The protocol consequence, delivered with the v5 campaign: mGGA-family architectures train and evaluate their truncated SCF from their own rung baseline (converged SCAN) instead of PBE, per the campaign decision record. **Why:** the density-footing asymmetry -- every rung's 3-cycle densities were scored "3 NN steps from PBE's fixed point" while SCAN comparison numbers use converged SCAN SCF, a handicap concentrated on the beyond-GGA arms whose teacher's fixed point was never their start. The Letter's own machinery preps per-campaign converged PBE-or-SCAN baselines (its training then blends toward a minao guess over 25 unrolled steps, and its benchmark evals are minao cold starts run to convergence; CCSD densities are labels only, never seeds), so per-rung baseline prep replicates the paper's campaign switch; the blend/cold-start training regime is recorded separately as the deferred B-regime comparison. The loss_vxc collocation deliberately STAYS at the PBE density for all arms: with D0 = SCAN for the mgga arms the L5 loss becomes mixed-footing -- the trajectory channels score a SCAN-seeded trajectory while loss_vxc remains collocated at the PBE density where the Wu-Yang reference is best conditioned; in v4 the collocation coincided with the trajectory's cycle-0 density, in v5-mgga it does not. A deliberate, uniform-across-arms residual asymmetry; the dm_target-collocation alternative is recorded as a deferred controlled experiment.
- 2026-08-14 -- **Cold-start trajectory-diagnostic channel (`eval_holdout_coldstart`).** Each spec's held-out eval can now run a FOURTH pass on the FINAL checkpoint under a shared cold-start override: functional-free minao seed, 25 SCF cycles (the Letter's step count), conv_tol 1e-12 so the latched |dE| freeze never masks the trajectory; mode stays FULL and every trained knob (mixer, tail loss, orientation lock, DF) is preserved. Off by default (`eval_coldstart: true` enables it); the override is applied in ONE helper by both the orchestrator (before the parallel/serial dispatch, so the in-process serial tiers inherit it) and the shard workers (via `--coldstart`, since they reload the spec pickle themselves). Every channel now writes an `eval_metadata.json` provenance stamp (the per-row cycles_run/scf_converged columns cannot distinguish a cold pass from a capped warm one). Completed runs get the channel retroactively via `cluster.coldstart_retro` (+ `hpcjobs/coldstart_holdout.sbatch`): completed = model.eqx present, done = the channel's per_reaction.json present, done specs skipped, so the job is resumable and safe beside running arrays. The channel is registered with the verbatim-rule refinalizer, the pull filter (+ rsync canary), and the figure labels; `plot_scf_convergence` reads it via `--eval-subdir`. **Why:** the Letter's benchmark evaluations are minao cold starts run to convergence under PySCF DIIS -- this repo's warm-seeded truncated channels never measure that capability at all. The v1 channel is a TRAJECTORY DIAGNOSTIC, not a converged-eval replica: the manual solver has linear mixing only, so 25 recorded cycles show whether a functional is walking toward a fixed point (the recorded 15-25-cycle mgga oscillation is expected phenomenology here); a DIIS mixer is the recorded follow-up that would close the remaining gap to the Letter's protocol.
- 2026-08-14 -- **v5 campaign defined: the SCAN-seeded retrain of the five meta-GGA-family architectures.** Two new arm YAMLs (`dfs_step7.dfs6311_grid3_v5.yaml`: deep_mgga / deep_mgga_attn / deep_rung35_mgga; `...v5mgga2.yaml`: deep_cusp_mgga / deep_rung35ms_mgga; 33 + 22 = 55 cells), byte-derived from their v4 arms with exactly five deltas -- `seed_xc: auto` (every arch in these arms resolves to the SCAN seed through the registry predicate; verified by executing the loader + per-cell resolution), `seed_cache_dir`, `eval_coldstart: true`, a fresh output root, and `eval_time` raised for the fourth 25-cycle channel. Losses, solver, subsets, hyperparameters, inputs, and the pretrain block are untouched, so v4-vs-v5 on these architectures is a controlled seed A/B with the v4 rows remaining as the PBE-seeded control; the shared pretrain npz cannot regenerate (the staleness gate keys on unchanged fields). The training-side seed cache is generated by `cluster.seed_cache` + `hpcjobs/seed_cache_scan.sbatch`: species enumerated programmatically from the config's own pool builder (measured: 32 = 23 molecules + 21-AE/CH4/HF composition, 7 atoms, 2 IP13 cations; `ae_as_reactions` is load-bearing for the atom set), one converged SCAN SCF each at the run identity including the orientation lock; the scan-pool intermediates are linked in for the val/eval species (214/214 already cached). Sequence: seed-cache job before the arm submits, one-spec smoke first. **Why:** phase 1 of the seeding A/B decided for the campaign -- per-rung baseline seeding at the established full_3 protocol, with the pure-GGA and pure rung-3.5 v4 rows carrying over unchanged (PBE is their baseline under the phase-1 policy). REVISION (same day): the in-flight v4 mgga arm was cancelled mid-array rather than run to completion as a PBE-seeded control -- the mis-seeded mgga trainings were judged not worth their remaining queue budget, so v5 is the sole mgga record; the completed v4 mgga cells remain on disk but are retired from comparison, and the merged figure view excludes the v4 mgga arms rather than labeling them as a control series. A deliberate PBE-seeded mgga control, if ever wanted, is its own future submission.
- 2026-08-14 -- **Seed cache identity geometry-qualified: same-name twins separated.** The first production seed-cache run exposed a structural flaw the overlap fingerprint could only fail loudly on, not fix: cache filenames keyed on the bare species name, and four training species collided with linked pool entries (measured on the job's own artifacts: 242 files where 246 were expected; H2, H2O, CH4 answered training lookups with POOL-geometry dms -- max deviations 4.6e-4, 0.76, and 1.26 Angstrom -- while the atom O was a genuinely shared geometry). Training needs those names at the G2/97 geometries and the scan-seeded held-out eval needs the same names at the pool geometries, which one filename can never host. Seed cache names now carry an 8-hex geometry tag hashed from the CANONICALIZED atom string (coordinates at the atoms_to_pyscf_str 1e-6 Angstrom precision, so producer formatting differences hash identically) plus charge and spin: twins separate, identical geometries deduplicate to one file, and the overlap fingerprint remains as the belt behind the filename (a tampered npz under the correct qualified name is still refused). The seed-cache driver qualifies its generation entries through the same shared canonicalization and gains a --link-pool phase that re-keys the scan-pool intermediates under pool-geometry-qualified names (replacing the sbatch's name-blind symlink loop); the val-coverage gate inherits the qualification through the shared path helper. **Why:** without this, every v5 mgga training task containing H2/H2O/CH4 would have died at the fingerprint check on startup -- and the cache design would have had no consistent state to heal to, since generation under the plain name could only reproduce the collision.
- 2026-08-15 -- **Merged figure view validates seed provenance; verbatim-rule parity closed at zero delta.** The pull/merge roster moves to the v4gga arm plus the two SCAN-seeded v5 arms (the retired v4 mgga arms are excluded per the cancellation record), and the merged view no longer trusts the roster for correctness: before an arm enters, every registry architecture it carries has its resolved seed checked against the rung-baseline policy and a mismatch refuses the merge outright -- the retired PBE-seeded mgga protocol, replayed verbatim from its own YAML, is refused with the policy message; an arm whose provenance cannot be verified is likewise refused, and identical (arch, subset size) cells arriving from two arms are refused as double-counts. Separately, the closing parity probe on the repulled v4gga run reported 54 parity / 0 stale-rule / 0 value-mismatch with max delta 0.0 on every row across 27 specs and both channels -- including the 8 specs evaluated natively under the deployed rule after the refinalization, so cluster writes and the local reconstruction are demonstrably one path. **Why:** grouped figures must be structurally unable to ingest mis-seeded or double-counted data, rather than protected only by the arm list being right; the zero-delta parity closes the 2026-08-13 hold-out redefinition loop end to end.
- 2026-08-14 -- **local_reeval replay drift fixed.** The local re-evaluation script's precompute passed neither the DF `auxbasis` nor the `orientation_lock_strength`, and requested the full `eri` even for density-fitted specs (which need the 3-index `cderi`). Replays of DF/locked specs therefore ran on a different Coulomb identity and an unlocked h_core -- or crashed on the missing cderi. The precompute-kwargs derivation is now a shared pure helper mirroring the training batch builder exactly, seed fields included. **Why:** a replay that does not reproduce the training identity is not a replay; the drift predated the seeding work and was surfaced by the seed-identity review.


## Phase 38 -- v4gga pull review: reference-guard attribution, pooled-baseline disclosure, comparator glyphs (2026-08-17)

- 2026-08-17 -- **Reference-consistency guards name the outlier spec; pooled baselines disclose a reduced pool.** A review of the 2026-08-17 v4gga pull traced every energy/density reference disagreement to a single degraded eval: spec_0042's (deep_rung35_3x16, ss18) final-checkpoint holdout eval hit tier-1 parallelism degradation and the 2-thread retry re-converged the c2 PBE SCF to a different solution (-75.781328 vs the 42-spec value -75.757329 Ha, 24 mHa; its density reference moved identically: 0.00200408 vs 0.00250095), while the same spec's best/val-best channels agree with all other specs -- a per-eval SCF multi-solution artifact on the multireference C2 dimer, not the cross-arm reference drift the guard message suggested. The energy guard's warning now clusters the per-spec values within its tolerance and names the disagreeing spec(s) ("outlier eval(s): spec_0042 @ ... vs 42 specs @ ... -- re-evaluate the outlier spec(s)"; an even split reports "multi-spec split" with every side named), dropping the "pending reference repair" phrase; the density guard carries the same attribution tagged with the tripping channel. The pooled PBE/SCAN baselines now record coverage against the UNRESTRICTED canonical leg (76/140/216 reactions), so a guard exclusion surfaces as "[215/216 reactions]" on the console/footers and ", 215/216" on the reference-line labels instead of silently averaging a smaller pool -- the final-checkpoint figure set had been quoting a 215-reaction PBE/SCAN baseline (W4-11 13.82 / SCAN combined 4.79) beside the val-best set's 216-reaction values (14.01 / 4.89) with no on-figure indication that the pools differ (w411_c2_atomization is the only c2 reaction; it sits in the validation slice, so the NN cells never carried it either way). Full-coverage output is byte-identical to before. The docstring claim that the best-checkpoint channel carries the same PBE energies is corrected (each channel pools its own per_molecule files and can exclude different molecules). Detection signal: cross-spec E_pbe spread > 1e-4 Ha (density: > 5% relative). Remedy for the data itself: re-run spec_0042's eval_holdout on the cluster (idempotent re-eval); the disclosure self-clears once the references agree.
  **Why:** an exclusion that changes the quoted baseline pool must be visible where the numbers are read, and a warning that misattributes a one-eval artifact to reference drift sends the repair at the wrong target (the reference cache is intact; one eval is not). The review also confirmed the pull's other exclusions exact: the 13 NaN-dropped reconstruction rows are 11 species of the same spec_0042 eval (9 test + 4 validation reactions, all finite in best/val-best -- the 2253-vs-2257 validation-row difference between the channels is exactly the 4 NaN validation rows, counted NaN-first), and the 85 strict-holdout density drops reproduce element-for-element from the per-spec trained lists under the alias layer (per-arch subset pattern 0,1,1,2,1,1,2,2,3,3,7 = 23; 3x23 + 16 for the arch missing ss26).

- 2026-08-17 -- **Cell-matched comparator marks redrawn as capped per-group spans with an explicit key; the per-arch MAE figure's reference lines row-matched.** The per-cell PBE/SCAN comparator marks (the anchors behind every beats verdict since the 2026-08-13 anchor-fairness repair) were per-bar tick dashes that read as bar clutter while carrying the load: on the current 43-cell v4gga view the cell-matched-vs-pooled deviation is under 2% on 79 of 86 WTMAD-2 ED cells (per-leg medians 0.1-0.5%, maxima 4-6%), yet verdicts flip at the margin -- on the headline combined-ED CSV, 3 final-view flips and 0 val-best; across every written CSV leg, 9 final-view and 3 val-best (the val-best flips live in the per-channel 3x3 CSV's BH76 WTMAD-2 leg) -- so the marks stay. Each subset-size group now carries ONE capped horizontal span (error-bar style: a horizontal segment across the group's bar cluster with vertical end caps demarking its extent) at the group's shared cell-matched value, black PBE / grey SCAN -- the group's cells score the same test slice, so their anchors agree to fp summation noise and one span carries what four overlapping per-bar marks smeared; a group whose cells disagree beyond 1e-6 relative (a degraded eval scored a different row set, >= 1e-3 relative in practice) falls back to per-bar spans so the divergence stays visible. The pooled PBE reference on these panels is drawn dash-dot (the thick dashed line read as another data element beside the spans; SCAN stays dotted), and every figure that draws the spans -- the WTMAD/MAE panels, the RMSE overview/3x3, and the DFS-units overview/3x3 twins, the last two of which had carried no key at all -- states the key explicitly for both PBE and SCAN in its footer, sourced from whichever panel's anchor map is populated. The ED combined/decomposition figures draw lines and scatter, not bars: their notes now point at the cell-matched CSV columns (ED_pbe_cell/ED_scan_cell) instead of describing glyphs they do not carry. Separately, the per-arch MAE summary figure had drawn a row-matched PBE line (deduped pulled rows) beside a full-pool SCAN line -- two different reaction sets inside one figure. Its SCAN line is now reduced over the same deduped rows when the cache covers at least 90% of THEM (labeled "SCAN row-matched MAE"; the gate is on the figure's own rows, so the row-matched line can draw even where the pooled line's own coverage floor would withdraw it), falling back to the full-pool value with its previous label when the cache is absent; the row-matched value also becomes the beats reference for the beyond-GGA archs there (4.79 -> 4.36 on the current pull, a stricter bar; zero verdicts move), superseding the 2026-08-13 full-pool carve-out for this figure -- the carve-out now covers the rung-summary and learning-curve figures only. Its PBE line switches to the shared deduped reduction (pbe_reaction_mae_baseline) rather than a spec-multiplicity-weighted inline mean (11.87 -> 11.97 on the current pull), and an on-figure note states that the reference lines are reduced over the figure's own rows while the grey footer's PBE/SCAN values are full-pool.
  **Why:** a comparator mark whose deviation from the pooled line is invisible on most cells but flips verdicts at the margin must stay legible without dominating the panel, and its meaning must be stated on every figure that draws it; a reference line pair averaging two different reaction sets inside one panel is the same defect the cell-matched anchors were introduced to close, and two values for one functional on one figure need their difference stated where they are read.

- 2026-08-17 -- **Merge and coverage provenance: unlabeled-spec states warn, spec integrity fields carried with their source run, IN PROGRESS split from NOT TRAINED.** The v5/v5mgga2 arms' newest runs currently hold pretrain output only -- no manifest.json -- which made the merged view's seed-provenance validation return silently with nothing to check. The merge now warns whenever spec dirs would merge unlabeled: an arm with on-disk specs but no usable manifest entries, AND the sneakier case of a readable manifest covering only part of the on-disk spec dirs (the uncovered rest previously merged with no labels, no duplicate-cell key, and no seed validation, silently); an arm that has not materialized anything yet gets a low-key note instead of the WARNING, so the loud message keeps its signal on routine pulls. The merged manifest entries carry the arm manifests' spec_file/sha256 fields through plus arm_run (the source run name), so a verifier can resolve <arm>/runs/<arm_run>/specs/<spec_file> against the recorded expected hash instead of guessing the newest run (which moves as pulls land; the fields were previously dropped entirely). MERGED_ARMS.txt rows gain an eval_holdout count beside the spec-dir count (the v4gga run holds 66 spec dirs of which 43 carry eval output; the bare dir count reads as an eval-cell count otherwise). The figure coverage note distinguishes mid-training cells: a spec whose only on-disk evidence is a resume checkpoint (resume_state.pkl / resume_*.eqx, no model.eqx, no completion sentinel -- the completion.json gate matches the harness resume predicates) reports its arch as "IN PROGRESS (resume checkpoint, no final weights yet)" instead of "NOT TRAINED in this run" -- on the 2026-08-17 pull, deep_rung35_3x16/ss26 and deep_rung35_attn_3x16/ss1 hold live resume checkpoints while the note called the latter arch not trained. The standalone enhancement-factor figure's weights-gated caption is relabeled "NO FINAL WEIGHTS in this run" (it cannot distinguish not-in-sweep / not-started / mid-training, and no longer claims to).
  **Why:** a guard that silently no-ops on exactly the arms it was built for offers only the appearance of validation, and a roster note that reports a running array as absent misdirects the campaign status read; the changes are disclosure-only (no numeric output changes).

- 2026-08-18 `eaecb07fc`, `6efd242d5` -- **Comparator anchors reduce the full test slice; incomplete NN coverage disclosed with starred bars.** The cell-matched PBE/SCAN anchors (the capped spans, the beats verdicts, the ED_*_cell CSV columns) previously reduced over the rows each cell SCORED: the figure-layer reconstruction dropped a reaction whenever either energy leg was non-finite, so an NN SCF failure removed the row from the COMPARATOR set too. On the 2026-08-18 pull exactly three specs carry non-finite NN energies (spec_0042 deep_rung35_3x16/ss18: 11 species, final channel only; spec_0044/spec_0045 deep_rung35_attn_3x16 ss1/ss2: 9/7 species final, 8/5 val-best), and the lost rows sit off the slice mean (dropped-set PBE MAE 3.90-11.91 vs slice means 11.21-12.03 kcal/mol), so those cells' anchors moved by up to 0.30 kcal/mol -- upward in 8 of the 10 affected (view, channel, cell) instances, the headline case being merged val-best ss2 at 11.882 vs the group's 11.582, downward in the other two -- and the one-span-per-group rendering fell back to per-bar spans at ss1/ss2 (val-best) and ss18 (final) -- the divergence fallback firing on a divergence the figure layer itself had manufactured; the defined holdout slice is provably identical across architectures at a fixed subset size (size-keyed subset ledger; identical trained-molecule records), and the cluster's own test_set.csv already reports one shared mae_pbe across all specs. The reconstruction now requires a finite COMPARATOR (PBE) leg only and keeps NN-failed reactions with NaN NN columns -- the convention the cluster-written per_reaction.json rows always carried, so the two ingest paths are now one semantics. Every per-cell comparator reduction (PBE/SCAN energy anchors, the density anchors -- now keyed on the comparator map rather than finite-NN rows, values unchanged since only the 15 reference-free atoms are NaN and in both legs -- and the derived ED anchors) covers the cell's full slice; at a fixed subset size the group anchors again agree to SCF-reconvergence noise by construction (up to 1.1e-11 relative measured across views, channels, and pool legs -- each spec's eval re-converged its own PBE SCFs), and the per-bar fallback remains as a detector of genuinely degraded COMPARATOR data (a NaN or drifted E_pbe). NN metrics keep reducing the rows the NN scored; a cell whose NN scored fewer reactions than its slice carries a star above the bar, is named in the note band with scored/slice counts and the missing reactions (the energy twin of the density coverage check), and is machine-readable in the ED CSVs as n_reactions < n_reactions_slice (new column). The subset-aggregate parity panel pairs its x=mean(ref)/y=mean(de_nn) over the same scored rows. Verified against the cluster's own accounting on spec_0044: the local W4-11 slice anchor reproduces test_set.csv's mae_pbe (13.983036) to the file's 6-decimal precision; on BH76 the local slice and the cluster per_reaction.json carry the identical 50-row multiset (the validation-twin exclusions live in the validation slice and never reach that file), so the raw-row mean equals the csv value (7.416204) while the figure's name-deduplicated anchor (48 names, 7.488727) reconciles with it by exactly the two duplicated BH76 pool entries: 48 x 7.488727 + 7.245262 + 4.106016 = 50 x 7.416204. Clean-cell numbers are bit-identical to the prior render, so previously quoted values stand. The NN reducers' name-dedup now tests per-key finiteness before consuming the name slot (matching the count helpers), so a NaN first instance of a duplicated pool name can never discard its finite twin -- unreachable on the current data (the four duplicated entries are byte-identical in stoichiometry and reference, and zero finite values were discarded across all views) but pinned by test. The three degraded evals themselves are queued for idempotent re-evaluation (reeval_holdout_spec.sbatch, spec indices 42/44/45); NaN species that reproduce under re-eval are genuine attention-architecture low-subset SCF divergences and stay disclosed by the same machinery.
  **Why:** a reference functional's error on a fixed holdout slice is a property of the slice, not of which rows one architecture's SCF happened to converge -- grading policy: the comparator baseline is always the full-slice reduction, never a single arch's surviving set, and an incomplete eval is marked rather than silently absorbed into a shifted reference. The same-set fairness concern that motivated cell-matched anchors (2026-08-13) was about exclusion-driven slice differences, which remain honored; NN-convergence-driven shrinkage rode along through the shared row gate and inverted the intent by letting the model move its own reference.

- 2026-08-18 -- **Local backfill of the NN-NaN holdout species: 52 of 53 entries repaired under identity gates (`notebooks/analysis/backfill_holdout_nans.py`).** The three degraded specs' evals carried 53 species-channel entries with null `E_total_nn` (spec_0042: 11 in the final channel; spec_0044: 9/7/8 and spec_0045: 7/6/5 across final/best/val-best) while the same rows' density columns were fully populated and `cycles_run` read 0 -- the signature of per-species evaluations killed transiently inside cluster shard processes (tier-1 parallelism RSS pressure), not of model divergence. The new driver recomputes exactly those species locally through the cluster's own shard worker (`workers.eval_holdout_worker.compute_shard`: spec pickle -> converged-PBE seed + density fitting + orientation lock -> truncated 3-cycle NN SCF -> the shared record writer), so no physics is reimplemented, and patches ONLY the energy/SCF fields of `per_molecule.json` -- the recorded `E_pbe` and every density column stay verbatim (the CCSD benchmark reference densities are not staged locally, so a full row rewrite would null them). Two identity gates guard every patch, both anchored to measurement: per channel, three finite control species must reproduce their recorded `E_total_nn`/`E_pbe` (measured across the seven NaN-carrying channels plus the pilot: max |dE_nn| = 3.2e-12 Ha, max |dE_pbe| = 6.8e-13 Ha -- machine-precision replay of the cluster eval, open-shell controls included); per species, the locally converged PBE must match the recorded value within 1e-7 Ha (two decades above the ~1e-9 conv-tol reproducibility bound, five below the multi-solution signal). The gate fired exactly once: c2 in spec_0045/val-best converged 5.93e-2 Ha from the recorded PBE solution (the known multireference-C2 multi-solution class), so its NN energy was refused rather than seeded from the wrong density -- it stays null, with zero figure impact (c2's only reaction sits in the validation slice). One species (c3h7ts, spec_0045/final) was dropped by its first worker process sharing three transition-state structures -- reproducing the cluster's own transient failure mode locally -- and evaluated cleanly on retry in its own process; the incident added unresolved-target reporting and per-chunk work-file tags to the driver. Repair doctrine matches the house tools: idempotent (done = no null `E_total_nn`), once-only `per_molecule.pre_backfill.json` backup, atomic replace, a `backfill_meta.json` stamp per channel (gates, control deltas, per-species outcomes), and every computed payload banked in `backfill_ledger.json` so a cluster pull that overwrites the patch re-applies instantly without recomputation. Derived files were regenerated through `refinalize_verbatim`: the seven patched channels (spec_0042's best/val-best carried no NaN and were untouched) plus 63 stale-rule channels on 21 other specs whose on-disk `per_reaction.json`/`test_set.csv` still predated the verbatim-holdout rule (a figure-invisible canonicalization -- the figure layer reconstructs from `per_molecule.json`). Post-repair hardening, each pinned by test: a channel with hold-out targets but no finite control species refuses to patch (and the command line refuses fewer than 2 controls); a ledger whose coverage is stale or entirely gate-rejected falls through to a fresh computation instead of reporting the channel done; stamps merge across passes so a retry extends rather than erases the per-species outcomes (the ledger is the complete species bank either way); an unresolved target -- a species the worker returned nothing for -- exits nonzero, while gate skips and measured local divergences remain documented terminal outcomes. spec_0044's regenerated `test_set.csv` reads `n_dropped_nan = 0` with `mae_pbe` unchanged (7.416204/13.983036), confirming the comparator was slice-reduced all along and only the NN coverage was repaired.
  **Why:** the re-evaluations are minutes-scale per species and need no cluster round-trip; recomputing only the failed species under executable identity gates repairs the record without touching anything that was already sound, and a wrong-solution PBE seed is structurally excluded rather than trusted. The starred-bar/coverage-note disclosure machinery introduced with the slice anchors self-clears wherever the backfill landed and keeps naming what remains (c2).

- 2026-08-17 -- **Parity split by reaction class; density-parity legend.** New ablation_parity_by_class.png in the per-arm suite: a 2x3 grid -- W4-11 atomization | BH76 barriers | total columns; by-architecture (each arch's largest-subset spec, PBE as grey x) over by-training-subset-size (all cells, one shared colorbar) rows -- predicted vs benchmark reference on per-class square limits (BH76's +-150 kcal/mol scale is not crushed by W4-11's 0..1300), assembled from the existing parity machinery (_parity_scatter / _pool_parity_limits). Separately, the per-species density-parity-by-channel figure colored every point by architecture with no legend anywhere on the figure; it now carries a rung-ordered arch legend (both the RMSE original and the DFS-units twin).
  **Why:** barrier-height parity had no dedicated panel beside the AE one, and the class split on shared per-class frames shows where each architecture's error concentrates at both the per-arch and per-subset views; an arch-colored figure without a key is not readable.

## Phase 39 -- 2026-08-20 pull: held-out NaN pathway traced and the scheduler repaired; log-y figure siblings; the meta-GGA read against SCAN exposes the open-shell spin-scaling defect and the pretraining-fidelity gap; every in-flight training cancelled (2026-08-20)
